[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\DTOs\BaseDTO.php]

<?php

namespace App\DTOs;

abstract class BaseDTO
{
    abstract public function toArray(): array;

    public static function getMetadata(): array
    {
        return [];
    }

    /** use with Validator::make($data, XxxDTO::rules()) — return field => 'required|string|...' */
    public static function rules(): array
    {
        $out = [];
        foreach (static::getMetadata() as $field => $meta) {
            $out[$field] = $meta['rules'] ?? '';
        }
        return $out;
    }

    /** set to Frontend to render form */
    public static function uiSchema(): array
    {
        $out = [];
        foreach (static::getMetadata() as $field => $meta) {
            $out[$field] = [
                'input'    => $meta['input']    ?? 'text',
                'ui'       => $meta['ui']       ?? null,
                'params'   => $meta['params']   ?? [],
                'required' => $meta['required'] ?? false,
                'default'  => $meta['default']  ?? null,
            ];
        }
        return $out;
    }

    public function toJson(int $flags = JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE): string
    {
        return json_encode($this->toArray(), $flags);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\DTOs;

final class OrderDTO extends BaseDTO
{
    public function __construct(
        public readonly ?string $order_nr = null,
        public readonly ?int $product_id = null,
        public readonly ?string $quantity = '1',
        public readonly ?bool $confirm_order = false,
    ) {}

    public static function fromArray(array $data): self
    {
        return new self(
            order_nr: $data['order_nr'] ?? null,
            product_id: $data['product_id'] ?? null,
            quantity: $data['quantity'] ?? '1',
            confirm_order: $data['confirm_order'] ?? false,
        );
    }

    public function toArray(): array
    {
        return [
            'order_nr' => $this->order_nr,
            'product_id' => $this->product_id,
            'quantity' => $this->quantity,
            'confirm_order' => $this->confirm_order,
        ];
    }

    public static function getMetadata(): array
    {
        return [
            'order_nr' => [
                'type' => 'string',
                'php_type' => 'string',
                'input' => 'text',
                'ui' => NULL,
                'params' => [
                    'length' => 255
                ],
                'required' => false,
                'default' => NULL,
                'rules' => 'nullable|string|max:255'
            ],
            'product_id' => [
                'type' => 'foreign',
                'php_type' => 'int',
                'input' => NULL,
                'ui' => NULL,
                'params' => [],
                'required' => false,
                'default' => NULL,
                'rules' => 'nullable|integer|exists:products,id'
            ],
            'quantity' => [
                'type' => 'decimal',
                'php_type' => 'string',
                'input' => 'number',
                'ui' => NULL,
                'params' => [
                    'total_digits' => 10,
                    'scale' => 2
                ],
                'required' => true,
                'default' => '1',
                'rules' => 'required|numeric|decimal:0,2|max:99999999.99'
            ],
            'confirm_order' => [
                'type' => 'boolean',
                'php_type' => 'bool',
                'input' => 'select',
                'ui' => NULL,
                'params' => [],
                'required' => false,
                'default' => false,
                'rules' => 'nullable|boolean'
            ],
        ];
    }
}

namespace App\DTOs;

        return new self(
            name: $data['name'] ?? null,
            image: $data['image'] ?? '',

            price: $data['price'] ?? '0',
            stock: $data['stock'] ?? '1',

            'image' => $this->image,

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\AuthenticatedSessionController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class AuthenticatedSessionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the login view.
     */
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/Login', [
            'canResetPassword' => Route::has('password.request'),
            'status' => session('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming authentication request.
     */
    public function store(LoginRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->authenticate();

        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    /**
     * Destroy an authenticated session.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();

        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/');
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\ConfirmablePasswordController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class ConfirmablePasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the confirm password view.
     */
    public function show(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/ConfirmPassword');
    }

    /**
     * Confirm the user's password.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if (! Auth::guard('web')->validate([
            'email' => $request->user()->email,
            'password' => $request->password,
        ])) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'password' => __('auth.password'),
            ]);
        }

        $request->session()->put('auth.password_confirmed_at', time());

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\EmailVerificationNotificationController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class EmailVerificationNotificationController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Send a new email verification notification.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
        }

        $request->user()->sendEmailVerificationNotification();

        return back()->with('status', 'verification-link-sent');
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\EmailVerificationPromptController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class EmailVerificationPromptController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the email verification prompt.
     */
    public function __invoke(Request $request): RedirectResponse|Response
    {
        return $request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()
                    ? redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false))
                    : Inertia::render('Auth/VerifyEmail', ['status' => session('status')]);
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\NewPasswordController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\PasswordReset;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class NewPasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset view.
     */
    public function create(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/ResetPassword', [
            'email' => $request->email,
            'token' => $request->route('token'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming new password request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'token' => 'required',
            'email' => 'required|email',
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        // Here we will attempt to reset the user's password. If it is successful we
        // will update the password on an actual user model and persist it to the
        // database. Otherwise we will parse the error and return the response.
        $status = Password::reset(
            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
            function ($user) use ($request) {
                $user->forceFill([
                    'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                    'remember_token' => Str::random(60),
                ])->save();

                event(new PasswordReset($user));
            }
        );

        // If the password was successfully reset, we will redirect the user back to
        // the application's home authenticated view. If there is an error we can
        // redirect them back to where they came from with their error message.
        if ($status == Password::PASSWORD_RESET) {
            return redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status));
        }

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => [trans($status)],
        ]);
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class PasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Update the user's password.
     */
    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'current_password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
            'password' => ['required', Password::defaults(), 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        $request->user()->update([
            'password' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
        ]);

        return back();
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\PasswordResetLinkController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class PasswordResetLinkController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset link request view.
     */
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/ForgotPassword', [
            'status' => session('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming password reset link request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'email' => 'required|email',
        ]);

        // We will send the password reset link to this user. Once we have attempted
        // to send the link, we will examine the response then see the message we
        // need to show to the user. Finally, we'll send out a proper response.
        $status = Password::sendResetLink(
            $request->only('email')
        );

        if ($status == Password::RESET_LINK_SENT) {
            return back()->with('status', __($status));
        }

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => [trans($status)],
        ]);
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\RegisteredUserController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class RegisteredUserController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the registration view.
     */
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/Register');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming registration request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|string|lowercase|email|max:255|unique:'.User::class,
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        $user = User::create([
            'name' => $request->name,
            'email' => $request->email,
            'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
        ]);

        event(new Registered($user));

        Auth::login($user);

        return redirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\Auth\VerifyEmailController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\EmailVerificationRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;

class VerifyEmailController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Mark the authenticated user's email address as verified.
     */
    public function __invoke(EmailVerificationRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
        }

        if ($request->user()->markEmailAsVerified()) {
            event(new Verified($request->user()));
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\BaseController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

abstract class BaseController extends Controller
{
    protected function responseSuccess(mixed $data, $message = 'Success')
    {
        return response()->json(['status' => 'success', 'data' => $data, 'message' => $message]);
    }

    protected function responseError(string $message, $code = 400)
    {
        return response()->json(['status' => 'error', 'message' => $message], $code);
    }
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\OrderController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\DTOs\OrderDTO;
use App\Models\Order;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class OrderController extends BaseController
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        return $this->responseSuccess(Order::all());
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate(OrderDTO::rules());
        $dto = OrderDTO::fromArray($request->all());
        return $this->responseSuccess(Order::create($dto->toArray()));
    }

    public function show(int $id)
    {
        return $this->responseSuccess(Order::findOrFail($id));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, $id)
    {
        $request->validate(OrderDTO::rules());
        $dto = OrderDTO::fromArray($request->all());
        $item = Order::findOrFail($id);
        $item->update($dto->toArray());
        return $this->responseSuccess($item);
    }

    public function destroy($id)
    {
        return Order::findOrFail($id)->delete();
    }
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\ProfileController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Requests\ProfileUpdateRequest;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Redirect;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class ProfileController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the user's profile form.
     */
    public function edit(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Profile/Edit', [
            'mustVerifyEmail' => $request->user() instanceof MustVerifyEmail,
            'status' => session('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Update the user's profile information.
     */
    public function update(ProfileUpdateRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->user()->fill($request->validated());

        if ($request->user()->isDirty('email')) {
            $request->user()->email_verified_at = null;
        }

        $request->user()->save();

        return Redirect::route('profile.edit');
    }

    /**
     * Delete the user's account.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
        ]);

        $user = $request->user();

        Auth::logout();

        $user->delete();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return Redirect::to('/');
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Controllers\RecordController.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use App\Services\RecordService;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;

class RecordController extends Controller
{
    protected RecordService $recordService;

    public function __construct(RecordService $recordService)
    {
        $this->recordService = $recordService;
    }

    /**
     * ```text
     * get all data from each Entity 
     * (e.g. /api/records/users or /api/records/products)
     */
    // public function index(string $entity)
    // {
    //     return response()->json($this->recordService->getAll($entity));
    // }
    public function index(string $entity)
    {
        Log::info('RecordController@index called', ['entity' => $entity]);

        try {
            $data = $this->recordService->getAll($entity);
            return response()->json($data);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::error('RecordController@index failed', [
                'entity' => $entity,
                'message' => $e->getMessage(),
                'file' => $e->getFile(),
                'line' => $e->getLine(),
            ]);
            throw $e;
        }
    }

    /**
     * create new store
     */
    public function store(Request $request, string $entity)
    {
        $id = $this->recordService->create($entity, $request->all());
        return response()->json(['id' => $id, 'success' => true]);
    }

    /**
     * update data
     */
    public function update(Request $request, string $entity, $id)
    {
        $success = $this->recordService->update($entity, $id, $request->all());
        return response()->json(['success' => $success]);
    }

    /**
     * delete data
     */
    public function destroy(string $entity, $id)
    {
        $success = $this->recordService->delete($entity, $id);
        return response()->json(['success' => $success]);
    }
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Middleware\HandleInertiaRequests.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Middleware;

class HandleInertiaRequests extends Middleware
{
    /**
     * The root template that is loaded on the first page visit.
     *
     * @var string
     */
    protected $rootView = 'app';

    /**
     * Determine the current asset version.
     */
    public function version(Request $request): ?string
    {
        return parent::version($request);
    }

    /**
     * Define the props that are shared by default.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function share(Request $request): array
    {
        return [
            ...parent::share($request),
            'auth' => [
                'user' => $request->user(),
            ],
        ];
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest.php]

<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Auth;

use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Lockout;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\RateLimiter;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class LoginRequest extends FormRequest
{
    /**
     * Determine if the user is authorized to make this request.
     */
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Get the validation rules that apply to the request.
     *
     * @return array<string, ValidationRule|array<mixed>|string>
     */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Attempt to authenticate the request's credentials.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function authenticate(): void
    {
        $this->ensureIsNotRateLimited();

        if (! Auth::attempt($this->only('email', 'password'), $this->boolean('remember'))) {
            RateLimiter::hit($this->throttleKey());

            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => trans('auth.failed'),
            ]);
        }

        RateLimiter::clear($this->throttleKey());
    }

    /**
     * Ensure the login request is not rate limited.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function ensureIsNotRateLimited(): void
    {
        if (! RateLimiter::tooManyAttempts($this->throttleKey(), 5)) {
            return;
        }

        event(new Lockout($this));

        $seconds = RateLimiter::availableIn($this->throttleKey());

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => trans('auth.throttle', [
                'seconds' => $seconds,
                'minutes' => ceil($seconds / 60),
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Get the rate limiting throttle key for the request.
     */
    public function throttleKey(): string
    {
        return Str::transliterate(Str::lower($this->string('email')).'|'.$this->ip());
    }
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Services\OrderService.php]

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\User;

class OrderService
{
    public function __construct(private readonly UserService $userService)
    {
    }

    public function canCreate(?User $user, array $payload): bool
    {
        if ($this->userService->isDev($user)) {
            return true;
        }

        return $user !== null
            && isset($payload['user_id'])
            && (string) $payload['user_id'] === (string) $user->id;
    }

    public function canUpdate(?User $user, ?array $shop): bool
    {
        if ($this->userService->isDev($user)) {
            return true;
        }

        return $user !== null
            && $shop !== null
            && isset($shop['user_id'])
            && (string) $shop['user_id'] === (string) $user->id;
    }

    public function canDelete(?User $user): bool
    {
        return $this->userService->isDev($user);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\User;

class ProductService
{
    public function __construct(private readonly UserService $userService)
    {
    }

    public function canCreate(?User $user, array $payload, ?array $shop): bool
    {
        if ($this->userService->isDev($user)) {
            return true;
        }

        return $user !== null
            && isset($payload['user_id'], $payload['shop_id'])
            && (string) $payload['user_id'] === (string) $user->id
            && $shop !== null
            && isset($shop['user_id'])
            && (string) $shop['user_id'] === (string) $user->id;
    }

    public function canUpdate(?User $user, array $product): bool
    {
        if ($this->userService->isDev($user)) {
            return true;
        }

        return $user !== null
            && isset($product['user_id'])
            && (string) $product['user_id'] === (string) $user->id;
    }

    public function canDelete(?User $user, array $product): bool
    {
        return $this->canUpdate($user, $product);
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Services\RecordService.php]

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Exception;

class RecordService
{
    /**
     * map entity name from frontend to Model in Laravel
     */
    protected function resolveModel(string $entity)
    {
        // change e.g. "users" -> "User", "products" -> "Product"
        $modelName = 'App\\Models\\' . Str::studly(Str::singular($entity));

        if (!class_exists($modelName)) {
            throw new Exception("Entity [{$entity}] not found.");
        }

        return new $modelName();
    }

    public function getAll(string $entity)
    {
        $model = $this->resolveModel($entity);
        return $model->all();
    }

    public function create(string $entity, array $data)
    {
        $model = $this->resolveModel($entity);
        $record = $model->create($data);
        return $record->id ?? $record->getKey();
    }

    public function update(string $entity, $id, array $data)
    {
        $model = $this->resolveModel($entity);
        $record = $model->find($id);

        if (!$record) return false;

        return $record->update($data);
    }

    public function delete(string $entity, int $id)
    {
        $model = $this->resolveModel($entity);
        $record = $model->find($id);

        if (!$record) return false;

        return $record->delete();
    }
}

            return true;

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\app\Services\UserService.php]

<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\User;
use RuntimeException;

class UserService
{
    public function findByEmail(string $email): User
    {
        $normalizedEmail = strtolower(trim($email));
        $user = User::query()->where('email', $normalizedEmail)->firstOrFail();

        return $this->assertActive($user);
    }

    public function canAccess(?User $user, string|int $ownerId): bool
    {
        if ($this->isDev($user)) {
            return true;
        }

        return $user !== null && (string) $user->id === (string) $ownerId;
    }

    public function isDev(?User $user): bool
    {
        if (!$user || !$user->is_active) {
            return false;
        }

        return in_array(strtolower($user->email), [
            'castermatt1@gmail.com',
            'kaybaancentro@gmail.com',
        ], true);
    }

    private function assertActive(User $user): User
    {
        if (!$user->is_active) {
            throw new RuntimeException('User account is inactive.');
        }

        return $user;
    }
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\ButtonSetting.jsx]

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import "./ButtonSetting.css";
import { L } from "@/i18n";
import { assignBackdrop } from "@/utils/backdrop";
import RecordProfile from "@/features/record/record.profile";
// import { deleteUserProfile } from "@/services/service.user"; // old
import { deleteUserProfile } from "@/features/user/user.service";
import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";

export default function ButtonSetting({ setTheme }) {
    const [showSettingItem, setShowSettingItem] = useState(false);
    const [showProfile, setShowProfile] = useState(false);
    const [openKey, setOpenKey] = useState(0);
    const user = useAuth();
    const ref = useRef(null);

    const deleteProfile = () => {
        deleteUserProfile(user?.uid);
    };

    const handleProfile = () => {
        setShowSettingItem(false);
        setShowProfile(true);
        setOpenKey((openCount) => openCount + 1);
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        function handleClickOutside(e) {
            if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target)) {
                setShowSettingItem(false);
            }
        }
        document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
        };
    }, []);

    return (
        <>
            {showProfile && (
                <RecordProfile
                    key={openKey} //ChatGPT : React need to see new value to reopen this
                    onClose={() => setShowProfile(false)}
                />
            )}

            {showSettingItem && (
                <div
                    className="setting-backdrop"
                    onClick={() => setShowSettingItem(false)}
                />
            )}

            {/* <div className="setting-backdrop" onClick={onClose} /> */}
            <button
                className={`btn-setting btn-icon-only ${
                    showSettingItem ? "is-active" : ""
                }`}
                onClick={() => {
                    setShowSettingItem((show) => !show);
                    assignBackdrop("setting-backdrop");
                }}
            >
                <span className="display-icon">⚙️</span>
            </button>

            {/* dropdown panel */}
            {showSettingItem && (
                <div className="setting-container">
                    <button
                        className="btn-setting-item"
                        onClick={() => {
                            setTheme((prev) =>
                                prev === "luxury" ? "root" : "luxury",
                            );
                            setShowSettingItem(false);
                        }}
                    >
                        🎨 {L.switch_theme}
                    </button>

                    {user && (
                        <button
                            className="btn-setting-item"
                            onClick={() => {
                                handleProfile();
                                setShowSettingItem(false);
                            }}
                        >
                            👤 {L.profile}
                        </button>
                    )}

                    {user && (
                        <button
                            className="btn-setting-item"
                            onClick={() => {
                                deleteProfile();
                                setShowSettingItem(false);
                            }}
                        >
                            ❌ {L.delete} {L.profile}
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\DangerButton.jsx]

export default function DangerButton({
    className = '',
    disabled,
    children,
    ...props
}) {
    return (
        <button
            {...props}
            className={
                `inline-flex items-center rounded-md border border-transparent bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-white transition duration-150 ease-in-out hover:bg-red-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500 focus:ring-offset-2 active:bg-red-700 ${
                    disabled && 'opacity-25'
                } ` + className
            }
            disabled={disabled}
        >
            {children}
        </button>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\DevProvider.jsx]

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import "./DevProvider.css";

const DevContext = createContext();

const isLocalhost = window.location.hostname === "localhost";

const getInitialDev = () => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("DEV_MODE");

    // priority:
    // 1. manual override (localStorage)
    if (saved !== null) return saved === "true";

    // 2. auto dev on localhost
    return isLocalhost;
};

export function DevProvider({ children }) {
    const [DEV_MODE, setDEV_MODE] = useState(getInitialDev);

    // sync localStorage
    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem("DEV_MODE", DEV_MODE);
    }, [DEV_MODE]);

    const toggle = () => {
        setDEV_MODE((v) => !v);
    };

    const setDEV_MODE_SAFE = (value) => {
        setDEV_MODE(!!value);
    };

    return (
        <DevContext.Provider
            value={{
                DEV: DEV_MODE, // 🔥 ใช้ชื่อ DEV ได้เลยในทั้งโปรเจกต์
                DEV_MODE, // (optional เผื่อบางที่ยังใช้)
                toggle,
                setDEV: setDEV_MODE_SAFE,
            }}
        >
            {/* UI toggle (ไม่เปลี่ยน CSS ของคุณ) */}
            <div
                className="dev-switch"
                onClick={toggle}
                style={{
                    display: isLocalhost || DEV_MODE ? "block" : "none",
                }}
            >
                <div className={`track ${DEV_MODE ? "on" : "off"}`}>
                    <div className="thumb" />
                </div>
            </div>

            {children}
        </DevContext.Provider>
    );
}

export function useDev() {
    return useContext(DevContext);
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\Dropdown.jsx]

import { Transition } from '@headlessui/react';
import { Link } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { createContext, useContext, useState } from 'react';

const DropDownContext = createContext();

const Dropdown = ({ children }) => {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

    const toggleOpen = () => {
        setOpen((previousState) => !previousState);
    };

    return (
        <DropDownContext.Provider value={{ open, setOpen, toggleOpen }}>
            <div className="relative">{children}</div>
        </DropDownContext.Provider>
    );
};

const Trigger = ({ children }) => {
    const { open, setOpen, toggleOpen } = useContext(DropDownContext);

    return (
        <>
            <div onClick={toggleOpen}>{children}</div>

            {open && (
                <div
                    className="fixed inset-0 z-40"
                    onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                ></div>
            )}
        </>
    );
};

const Content = ({
    align = 'right',
    width = '48',
    contentClasses = 'py-1 bg-white',
    children,
}) => {
    const { open, setOpen } = useContext(DropDownContext);

    let alignmentClasses = 'origin-top';

    if (align === 'left') {
        alignmentClasses = 'ltr:origin-top-left rtl:origin-top-right start-0';
    } else if (align === 'right') {
        alignmentClasses = 'ltr:origin-top-right rtl:origin-top-left end-0';
    }

    let widthClasses = '';

    if (width === '48') {
        widthClasses = 'w-48';
    }

    return (
        <>
            <Transition
                show={open}
                enter="transition ease-out duration-200"
                enterFrom="opacity-0 scale-95"
                enterTo="opacity-100 scale-100"
                leave="transition ease-in duration-75"
                leaveFrom="opacity-100 scale-100"
                leaveTo="opacity-0 scale-95"
            >
                <div
                    className={`absolute z-50 mt-2 rounded-md shadow-lg ${alignmentClasses} ${widthClasses}`}
                    onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                >
                    <div
                        className={
                            `rounded-md ring-1 ring-black ring-opacity-5 ` +
                            contentClasses
                        }
                    >
                        {children}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Transition>
        </>
    );
};

const DropdownLink = ({ className = '', children, ...props }) => {
    return (
        <Link
            {...props}
            className={
                'block w-full px-4 py-2 text-start text-sm leading-5 text-gray-700 transition duration-150 ease-in-out hover:bg-gray-100 focus:bg-gray-100 focus:outline-none ' +
                className
            }
        >
            {children}
        </Link>
    );
};

Dropdown.Trigger = Trigger;
Dropdown.Content = Content;
Dropdown.Link = DropdownLink;

export default Dropdown;

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\Header.jsx]

import React from "react";
import "./Header.css";

export default function Header({ config, ButtonSetting }) {
    const title = config?.title ?? null;

    const centerItems = config?.actions
        ? React.Children.toArray(config.actions).flatMap((child) =>
              child?.props?.children ? child.props.children : child,
          )
        : [];

    return (
        <>
            {/* LEFT ZONE - Title */}
            {title}

            {/* CENTER ZONE - Actions */}
            <div className="header-content">
                {centerItems.map((child, i) => (
                    <React.Fragment key={i}>{child}</React.Fragment>
                ))}
            </div>

            {/* RIGHT ZONE - Settings Group */}
            <div className="setting">{ButtonSetting}</div>
        </>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\ImageForm.jsx]

import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { compressImage } from "@/utils/image";
import { forwardRef, useImperativeHandle } from "react";
import "./ImageForm.css";
import { L } from "@/i18n"; //import Language from host App, remove if not use L
import Preview from "@/components/Preview";

/**
 * RecordForm / ImageForm - IMAGE STATE MACHINE
 * ---------------------------------------------------------
 *
 * This component implements a deterministic image state system
 * for record create/edit flows.
 *
 * It supports 3 core states:
 *
 * 1. CASE A: First image upload (unsaved record)
 * 2. CASE B: Existing image (edit mode)
 * 3. CASE B1/B2: delete / replace transitions
 *
 * ---------------------------------------------------------
 * KEY DESIGN PRINCIPLE
 * ---------------------------------------------------------
 *
 * UI state is NOT the same as persisted DB state.
 *
 * - form?.image      → primary persisted/active image
 * - form?.image_new  → temporary replacement (UI-only)
 * - showDeleteOverlay → soft delete intention flag
 *
 * Only ONE final image is ever submitted.
 */

/**
 * React rule for pass parameters : 
 * export const ImageForm = forwardRef(({ prop }, ref) => {
        return ( some HTML );
    });
 * forwardRef must destructure props first, then ref is injected separately
 */
export const ImageForm = forwardRef(
    ({ form, image, updateField, setPreviewImage }, ref) => {
        /**
         * IMAGE STATE (derived)
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         *
         * currentImage:
         * → active image shown in UI
         * → source: form?.image
         *
         * newImage:
         * → replacement preview image (temporary state only)
         * → source: form?.image_new
         *
         * hasSavedImage:
         * → true if record already exists in DB
         *
         * hasUnsavedFirstImage:
         * → special CASE A detection (first upload before save)
         */

        const [showDeleteOverlay, setShowDeleteOverlay] = useState(false);

        const currentImage = form?.image;
        const newImage = form?.image_new;

        const hasSavedImage = Boolean(image);

        const hasUnsavedFirstImage = currentImage && !hasSavedImage;

        const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

        /**
         * IMAGE UPLOAD / REPLACEMENT HANDLER
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         *
         * Handles both:
         * - CASE A: first image upload
         * - CASE B2: replacement image upload
         */
        const handleImageChange = async (event) => {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;

            const compressedImage = await compressImage(file, 200);

            /**
             * Reset file input to allow re-upload of same file
             */
            event.target.value = "";

            /**
             * CASE A: First image upload
             * -----------------------------------------------------
             * No existing image → store directly into form?.image
             *
             * No image_new is created in this flow.
             */
            if (!currentImage) {
                updateField("image", compressedImage);
                return;
            }

            /**
             * CASE B2: Replacement image
             * -----------------------------------------------------
             * Create temporary preview state only
             *
             * image_new is introduced here as UI-only layer
             */
            updateField("image_new", compressedImage);
        };

        /**
         * IMAGE DELETE INTENT HANDLER
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         *
         * This does NOT immediately delete DB data.
         * It only sets user intent.
         */
        const handleDeleteImage = () => {
            /**
             * CASE A:
             * Unsaved first image
             * → delete immediately (no DB involvement)
             */
            if (currentImage && !image) {
                updateField("image", "");
                return;
            }

            /**
             * CASE B1:
             * Existing DB image
             * → mark for deletion (soft state)
             */
            setShowDeleteOverlay(true);
        };

        /**
         * UNDO STATE HANDLER
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         *
         * Restores previous image state depending on flow:
         * - removes replacement preview (image_new)
         * - clears delete overlay flag
         */
        const handleUndoDelete = () => {
            /**
             * CASE B2.2:
             * Remove replacement image (if exists)
             */
            if (newImage) {
                updateField("image_new", "");
            }

            /**
             * CASE B1:
             * Remove delete intent
             */
            setShowDeleteOverlay(false);
        };

        /**
         * TRIGGER FILE INPUT
         */
        const handleUploadImage = () => {
            fileInputRef.current.click();
        };

        /**
         * IMAGE RESOLUTION ENGINE (FINAL DECISION LAYER)
         * ---------------------------------------------------------
         *
         * This is the ONLY function used by RecordForm during submit.
         *
         * It resolves:
         * - delete state
         * - replacement state
         * - default state
         *
         * This replaces old inline logic:
         *
         * showDeleteOverlay ? "" : form?.image_new || form?.image
         */
        useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
            getResolvedImage: () => {
                /**
                 * CASE B1:
                 * DELETE OVERRIDES EVERYTHING
                 *
                 * No matter what exists in:
                 * - image
                 * - image_new
                 *
                 * result is always empty string
                 */
                if (showDeleteOverlay) return "";

                /**
                 * CASE B2:
                 * REPLACEMENT IMAGE TAKES PRIORITY OVER ORIGINAL
                 *
                 * image_new is temporary UI state only
                 */
                if (newImage) return newImage;

                /**
                 * CASE A / DEFAULT:
                 * fallback to original image
                 */
                return currentImage;
            },
        }));

        /**
         *  PARTS OF ImageForm
         */

        const CaseA_HAS_Image____overlay_CROSS = currentImage ? (
            <div className="ifm-container-wrapper">
                <img
                    className="ifm-img"
                    src={currentImage}
                    onClick={() => setPreviewImage(currentImage)}
                />

                {/* Overlay shown when image is inactive (delete or replaced) */}
                {(showDeleteOverlay || newImage) && (
                    <div className="ifm-cross-overlay">❌</div>
                )}
            </div>
        ) : (
            <div className="ifm-img ifm-no-image" onClick={handleUploadImage}>
                <span>{L.no_image}</span>
            </div>
        );

        const CaseA_NO_Image____btn_PLUS = !currentImage && (
            <button
                className="ifm-icon-only"
                type="button"
                onClick={handleUploadImage}
            >
                ➕
            </button>
        );

        const CaseA_HAS_Image____btn_CROSS = hasUnsavedFirstImage && (
            <button
                className="ifm-icon-only"
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDeleteImage}
            >
                ❌
            </button>
        );

        const CaseB_HAS_Image____btn_CROSS_REPLACE = hasSavedImage &&
            !showDeleteOverlay &&
            !newImage && (
                <>
                    <button
                        className="ifm-icon-only"
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleDeleteImage}
                    >
                        ❌
                    </button>

                    <button
                        className="ifm-icon-only"
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleUploadImage}
                    >
                        🖼️
                    </button>
                </>
            );

        const CaseB_HAS_Image____overlay_CROSS_btn_UNDONE =
            (showDeleteOverlay || newImage) && (
                <button
                    className="ifm-icon-only"
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleUndoDelete}
                >
                    ↩️
                </button>
            );

        const CaseB_NEW_Image____input_hidden = (
            <input
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                ref={fileInputRef}
                onChange={handleImageChange}
                hidden
            />
        );

        const CaseB_NEW_Image____img = image && (
            <>
                {newImage ? (
                    <img
                        className="ifm-img"
                        src={newImage}
                        alt="preview"
                        onClick={() => setPreviewImage(newImage)}
                    />
                ) : (
                    <div
                        className="ifm-img ifm-no-image"
                        onClick={handleUploadImage}
                    >
                        <span>{L.new_image}</span>
                    </div>
                )}
            </>
        );

        return (
            <>
                {/* IMAGE CONTAINER , need CSS from host App for background*/}
                <div className="ifm-container text-bg">
                    <div className="ifm-header">{L.upload_image}</div>

                    <div className="ifm-body">
                        {/* CURRENT IMAGE DISPLAY */}

                        {CaseB_NEW_Image____img}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </>
        );
    },
);

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\Modal.jsx]

import "./Modal.css";
import { withEscView } from "@/hooks/hoc.withEscView";
import useKeyEscape from "@/hooks/useKeyEscape";

function Modal({ children, onClose }) {
    useKeyEscape(onClose, Boolean(children), 100);
    return (
        <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
            <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>{children}</div>
        </div>
    );
}
/**when open preview from modal-form
 * and user hit Esc , then only preview close first
 * hit Esc again , then modal-form close
 */
export default withEscView(Modal);



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\Modal_old_laravel.jsx]

import {
    Dialog,
    DialogPanel,
    Transition,
    TransitionChild,
} from '@headlessui/react';

export default function Modal({
    children,
    show = false,
    maxWidth = '2xl',
    closeable = true,
    onClose = () => {},
}) {
    const close = () => {
        if (closeable) {
            onClose();
        }
    };

    const maxWidthClass = {
        sm: 'sm:max-w-sm',
        md: 'sm:max-w-md',
        lg: 'sm:max-w-lg',
        xl: 'sm:max-w-xl',
        '2xl': 'sm:max-w-2xl',
    }[maxWidth];

    return (
        <Transition show={show} leave="duration-200">
            <Dialog
                as="div"
                id="modal"
                className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex transform items-center overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 transition-all sm:px-0"
                onClose={close}
            >
                <TransitionChild
                    enter="ease-out duration-300"
                    enterFrom="opacity-0"
                    enterTo="opacity-100"
                    leave="ease-in duration-200"
                    leaveFrom="opacity-100"
                    leaveTo="opacity-0"
                >
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500/75" />
                </TransitionChild>

                <TransitionChild
                    enter="ease-out duration-300"
                    enterFrom="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
                    enterTo="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
                    leave="ease-in duration-200"
                    leaveFrom="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
                    leaveTo="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
                >
                    <DialogPanel
                        className={`mb-6 transform overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white shadow-xl transition-all sm:mx-auto sm:w-full ${maxWidthClass}`}
                    >
                        {children}
                    </DialogPanel>
                </TransitionChild>
            </Dialog>
        </Transition>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\Sidebar.jsx]

import { L } from "@/i18n";
import "./Sidebar.css";
import { PAGE } from "@/config/config.app";
import { useDev } from "@/components/DevProvider";
import { assignBackdrop } from "@/utils/backdrop";
import { login, logout, openChromeFromLine } from "@/services/service.auth";
import { usePermit } from "@/config/config.permit";
import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";
import { storeSpList } from "@/stores/store.global";

export default function Sidebar({
    handleNavigate,
    showSidebar,
    onToggle,
    setPage,
    setShowSidebar,
}) {
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    const { hasShop } = usePermit(PAGE.SHOP);
    const { hasProduct } = usePermit(PAGE.ORDER);

    const menuItems = [
        { label: L.product, value: "products", icon: "📦", permit: hasShop },

        { label: L.user, value: "users", icon: "⚙️", dev: true },
        {
            label: L.order,
            value: "orders",
            icon: "📜",
            permit: DEV || hasProduct,
        },
        { label: L.customer, value: "customers", icon: "👨‍👩‍👧‍👦" },
    ];

    function handleAccess() {
        setPage?.(null);
        setShowSidebar?.(true);
        storeSpList.getState().clearCart();
    }

    const user = useAuth();

    const handleClick = (value) => {
        handleNavigate(value);
        assignBackdrop("sidebar");
    };

    return (
        <div className="sidebar-container">
            {/* TOGGLE BUTTON */}
            <button className="sidebar-btn-menu" onClick={onToggle}>
                {showSidebar ? "☰" : "✕"}
            </button>
            {menuItems
                .filter((item) => {
                    // DEV ONLY
                    if (item.dev && !DEV) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    // PERMIT
                    if (item.permit === false) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    return true;
                })
                .map((item) => (
                    <button
                        key={item.value}
                        className="sidebar-btn-item"
                        onClick={() => handleClick(item.value)}
                    >
                        {item.icon}
                        {!showSidebar && (
                            <span className="label">{item.label}</span>
                        )}
                    </button>
                ))}

            {/* LOG OUT + SHOW BUTTON*/}
            {user && (
                <>
                    <button
                        className="sidebar-btn-item"
                        onClick={() => handleClick(PAGE.SHOP)}
                    >
                        🏠
                        {!showSidebar && (
                            <span className="label">{L.shop}</span>
                        )}
                    </button>

                    <button
                        className="sidebar-btn-item"
                        onClick={async () => {
                            await logout();
                            handleAccess();
                        }}
                    >
                        🚪
                        {!showSidebar && (
                            <span className="label">{L.logout}</span>
                        )}
                    </button>
                </>
            )}

            {/* LOG IN */}
            {!user && (
                <button
                    className="sidebar-btn-item"
                    onClick={async () => {
                        const ok = await openChromeFromLine();
                        if (!ok) return;
                        await login();
                        handleAccess();
                    }}
                >
                    🔑
                    {!showSidebar && <span className="label">{L.login}</span>}
                </button>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Components\TextInput.jsx]

import { forwardRef, useEffect, useImperativeHandle, useRef } from 'react';

export default forwardRef(function TextInput(
    { type = 'text', className = '', isFocused = false, ...props },
    ref,
) {
    const localRef = useRef(null);

    useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
        focus: () => localRef.current?.focus(),
    }));

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isFocused) {
            localRef.current?.focus();
        }
    }, [isFocused]);

    return (
        <input
            {...props}
            type={type}
            className={
                'rounded-md border-gray-300 shadow-sm focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-indigo-500 ' +
                className
            }
            ref={localRef}
        />
    );
});

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\config\config.firebase.rule.js]

rules_version = '2';

service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
  
    // This rule allows anyone with your Firestore database reference to view, edit,
    // and delete all data in your Firestore database. It is useful for getting
    // started, but it is configured to expire after 30 days because it
    // leaves your app open to attackers. At that time, all client
    // requests to your Firestore database will be denied.
    //
    // Make sure to write security rules for your app before that time, or else
    // all client requests to your Firestore database will be denied until you Update
    // your rules
    
    // (request.auth != null) login needed
    
    //every one can fire API without login 
    //match /{document=**} {
    //  allow read, write: if true // request.time < timestamp.date(2026, 5, 20);
    //}
  
  
    // DEV (god mode)
    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isDev() {
      return isSignedIn()
        && (
          request.auth.token.email == "castermatt1@gmail.com" ||
          request.auth.token.email == "kaybaancentro@gmail.com"
        );
    }

    // USERS (strict)

    match /users/{userId} {
        
      function isOwner() {
        return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
      }
      
      allow read: if isDev() || isOwner();

      allow update: if isDev() || isOwner();
      
      allow create: if isDev() || isOwner();

      allow delete: if isDev() || isOwner();
      
    }

    // SHOPS
    match /shops/{shopId} {
      allow read: if true;

      // create
      allow create: if
        isDev()
        || (
          request.auth != null
          && request.resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
        );

      // update
      allow update: if
        isDev()
        || (
          request.auth != null
          && resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
          && request.resource.data.user_id == resource.data.user_id
        );

      // delete
      allow delete: if
        isDev()
        || (
          request.auth != null
          && resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
        );
    }

    // SELECT_PRODUCTS
    match /select_products/{userId} {

      function isOwner() {
        return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
      }

      allow read: if isDev() || isOwner();

      allow create: if isDev() || isOwner();

      allow update: if isDev() || isOwner();

      allow delete: if isDev() || isOwner();
    }

    // PRODUCTS
    match /products/{productId} {

      allow read: if true;

      allow create: if isDev()
        || (
          request.auth != null
          && request.resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
          && exists(
            /databases/$(database)/documents/shops/$(request.resource.data.shop_id)
          )
          && get(
            /databases/$(database)/documents/shops/$(request.resource.data.shop_id)
          ).data.user_id == request.auth.uid
        );

      allow update, delete: if isDev()
        || (
          request.auth != null
          && resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
        );
    }
    
    // ORDER
    match /orders/{orderId} {
      allow read: if isDev()
        || (
          isSignedIn() 
          && (
            resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
            || get(/databases/$(database)/documents/shops/$(resource.data.shop_id)).data.user_id == request.auth.uid
          )
        );

      allow create: if isDev()
        || (
          isSignedIn()
          && request.resource.data.user_id == request.auth.uid
        );

      allow update: if isDev()
        || (
          isSignedIn()
          && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/shops/$(resource.data.shop_id)).data.user_id == request.auth.uid
        );

      allow delete: if isDev();
    }
    
  }
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\config\config.permit.js]

import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";
import { PAGE } from "@/config/config.app";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import useController from "@/features/record/record.controller";
import { productController } from "@/stores/store.global";

import {
    storeShop,
    storeProduct,
    storeUser,
    storeOrder,
} from "@/stores/store.global";

/**
 * usePermit make UI rules for all pages in this App
 * @param {*} PAGE_NAME
 * @param {*} controller
 * @returns
 */
export const usePermit = (PAGE_NAME, controller = null) => {
    const storeMap = {
        [PAGE.USER]: storeUser,
        [PAGE.SHOP]: storeShop,
        [PAGE.PRODUCT]: storeProduct,
        [PAGE.HOME]: storeShop,
        [PAGE.ORDER]: storeOrder,
    };

    const user = useAuth();

    if (!controller) {
        const store = storeMap[PAGE_NAME];

        controller = useController(store);
    }
    const records = controller?.records || [];

    const isPage_Shop = PAGE_NAME === PAGE.SHOP;
    const isPage_Product = PAGE_NAME === PAGE.PRODUCT;
    const isPage_Select = PAGE_NAME === PAGE.HOME;
    const isPage_Order = PAGE_NAME === PAGE.ORDER;

    /**
     *  SHOP
     */
    const hasShop = (() => {
        if (!isPage_Shop) return false;
        for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
            const shop = records[i];

            const shopOwnerId = shop?.[KEY.USER_ID];

            if (shopOwnerId === user?.uid) {
                return true; // found → exit immediately
            }
        }
        return false;
    })();

    const canAdd_Shop = isPage_Shop && !hasShop;

    /**
     *  PRODUCT
     */
    const canAdd_Product = isPage_Product && user?.uid;

    const hasProduct = (() => {
        if (!isPage_Order || !user) return false;

        const products = productController?.getRecords();
        const hasSomeProduct = products.some(
            (item) => item.user_id === user?.uid,
        );

        return hasSomeProduct;
    })();

    /**
     *  CAN ADD record , e.g. for showing add button
     *  should be used only in List not in Card
     *  because List makes Card
     *
     *  if filter isPage in Card , the result could be unpredictable
     */
    const canAdd = canAdd_Shop || canAdd_Product;

    if (isPage_Shop) {
        // console.log("records.length = ", records.length);
        // console.log("hasShop = ", hasShop);
        // console.log("canAdd_Shop = ", canAdd_Shop);
        // console.log("canAdd_Product = ", canAdd_Product);
    }

    return {
        canAdd,
        hasShop,
        hasProduct,
        isPage_Shop,
        isPage_Product,
        isPage_Select,
        isPage_Order,
    };
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\order\btn-order.jsx]

import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";
import storeSpList from "@/features/select/select.store";
import { productController } from "@/stores/store.global";
import { createOrder } from "@/features/order/order.service";
import { L } from "@/i18n";
import "./order.css";

export default function ButtonOrder() {
    const user = useAuth();

    // ดึงสเตตสินค้าในตะกร้าและฐานข้อมูลสินค้าหลักเพื่อนำมาแมปโครงสร้างภาพรวม
    const spItems = storeSpList((state) => state.select_product);
    const allProducts = productController.getRecords();
    const clearCart = storeSpList((state) => state.clearCart);

    const handleCheckout = async () => {
        if (!user?.uid) {
            alert(L.please_login);
            return;
        }

        const selectedProducts = spItems
            .map((spItem) => {
                const productDetail = allProducts.find(
                    (p) => p.id === spItem.product_id,
                );
                return {
                    ...productDetail,
                    qty: spItem.qty,
                };
            })
            .filter((p) => p.id);

        try {
            // save to db
            await createOrder(user.uid, selectedProducts);

            clearCart();

            alert(L.order_success);
        } catch (error) {
            alert(L.order_failure);
        }
    };

    return (
        <button
            className="btn-order btn-icon-only"
            onClick={handleCheckout}
            type="button"
        >
            📜
        </button>
    );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { L, formatCurrency, getCurrencyLabel } from "@/i18n";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { productController, shopController } from "@/stores/store.global";
import Preview from "@/components/Preview";
import "@/features/select/select.card.css";
import { useOwnerBadge } from "@/features/record/record.owner";
import { getUserById } from "@/features/user/user.service";

export default function OrderCard({ order }) {
    const [previewItem, setPreviewItem] = useState(null);

    const products = productController.getRecords();
    const product = products.find((p) => p.id === order?.product_id);
    const shop = shopController.getById(order?.shop_id);

    const qty = Number(order?.qty) || 0;
    const price = Number(product?.[KEY.PRICE]) || 0;
    const itemTotalPrice = price * qty;

    const ownerUser = getUserById(shop.user_id);
    const ownerName = ownerUser?.[KEY.NAME] || "Unknown";
    const shopName = shop.name;
    const photo = shop.image;
    const OWNER = useOwnerBadge(shopName, ownerName, photo, setPreviewItem);

    return (
        <>
            {previewItem && (
                <Preview
                    item={previewItem}
                    onClose={() => setPreviewItem(null)}
                    escapeEnabled={true}
                    escapePriority={100}
                />
            )}
            <div className="sp-card-container">
                <div className="sp-card-header text-bg">
                    {product?.[KEY.NAME] || "Unknown Product"}
                    <span
                        className="text-bg"
                        style={{
                            fontSize: "12px",
                            marginLeft: "10px",
                            color: "var(--color-primary)",
                        }}
                    >
                        {OWNER}
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div className="sp-card-body">
                    {product?.[KEY.IMAGE] ? (
                        <img
                            className="sp-img-card text-bg"
                            src={product[KEY.IMAGE]}
                            alt={product[KEY.NAME]}
                            onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                setPreviewItem(product[KEY.IMAGE]);
                            }}
                        />
                    ) : (
                        <div className="img-card text-bg no-image">
                            {L.no_image}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="sp-card-description text-bg">
                        <p className="label">
                            <span className="label-name">Price:</span>
                            <span className="label-value">
                                {formatCurrency(price)} {getCurrencyLabel()}
                            </span>
                        </p>
                        <p className="label">
                            <span className="label-name">
                                {L.piece || "Qty"}:
                            </span>
                            <span className="label-value">{qty}</span>
                        </p>
                        <p className="label total-price-row">
                            <span className="label-name">{L.total}:</span>
                            <span className="label-value">
                                {formatCurrency(itemTotalPrice)}{" "}
                                {getCurrencyLabel()}
                            </span>
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div
                        className="sp-card-action"
                        style={{
                            justifyContent: "center",
                            fontSize: "14px",
                            fontWeight: "bold",
                        }}
                    >
                        <span className="text-bg">
                            {L.status}:{" "}
                            {order?.status === "confirmed"
                                ? "✅ " + L.confirm_order
                                : "⏳ " + L.pending}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\order\order.list.jsx]

import {
    productController,
    storeUser,
    storeOrder,
} from "@/stores/store.global";
import { L, getCurrencyLabel, formatCurrency } from "@/i18n";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import OrderCard from "./order.card";
import "./order.css";

export default function OrderList({ user_id, order_number, onClose }) {
    const products = productController.getRecords();
    const users = storeUser((state) => state.records) || [];
    const customer = users.find((u) => u.id === user_id);
    const allOrderRecords = storeOrder((state) => state.records) || [];

    // Filter records by exact unique order_number
    const orderRecords = allOrderRecords.filter(
        (order) => order?.order_number === order_number,
    );

    // Calculate total amount for this order group
    const orderTotalAmount = orderRecords.reduce((totalSum, orderItem) => {
        const productDetail = products.find(
            (p) => p.id === orderItem?.product_id,
        );
        const qty = Number(orderItem?.qty) || 0;
        const price = Number(productDetail?.[KEY.PRICE]) || 0;
        return totalSum + price * qty;
    }, 0);

    const TOTAL_AMOUNT = orderTotalAmount > 0 && (
        <span className="sp-total-amount-product">
            {formatCurrency(orderTotalAmount)} {getCurrencyLabel()}
        </span>
    );

    return (
        <div className="sp-shell">
            <div className="sp-header">
                <div className="prod-count-total-price">
                    <span className="sp-header-shop-name text-bg">
                        {customer?.name}
                    </span>
                    {TOTAL_AMOUNT}
                </div>
                <span className="sp-count text-bg">
                    {orderRecords.length} รายการ
                </span>
                <button className="btn-modal-close" onClick={onClose}>
                    ✕
                </button>
            </div>

            <div className="sp-page-area">
                {orderRecords.map((order) => (
                    <OrderCard key={order.id} order={order} />
                ))}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\order\order.service.js]

import { createRecordService } from "@/services/service.record";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { storeSpList } from "@/stores/store.global";

const orderService = createRecordService("orders");

export async function createOrder(buyerUid, selectedProducts) {
    if (!buyerUid || !Array.isArray(selectedProducts) || selectedProducts.length === 0) {
        return;
    }

    try {
        const now = new Date();
        const yy = String(now.getFullYear()).slice(-2);
        const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
        const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
        const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, "0");
        const min = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
        const ss = String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, "0");
        const timeStampStr = `${yy}.${mm}.${dd}_${hh}.${min}.${ss}`;

        for (const product of selectedProducts) {
            const currentShopId = String(product?.shop_id || "UNKNOWN");
            const shortShop = currentShopId.slice(0, 4);
            const customOrderNumber = `${shortShop}_${timeStampStr}`;

            await orderService.addRecord({
                order_number: customOrderNumber,
                [KEY.USER_ID]: buyerUid,
                [KEY.PRODUCT_ID]: product.id,
                [KEY.QUANTITY]: Number(product?.qty) || 0,
                [KEY.SHOP_ID]: product?.shop_id ?? null,
            });
        }

        storeSpList.getState().clearCart();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("ERROR at create order:", error);
        throw error;
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\order\order.service_old.js]

import { db } from "@/config/config.firebase";
import { collection, addDoc, serverTimestamp } from "firebase/firestore";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { storeSpList } from "@/stores/store.global";

const COLLECTION = "orders";

export async function createOrder(buyerUid, selectedProducts) {
    if (!buyerUid || selectedProducts.length === 0) return;

    try {
        // make timestamp
        const now = new Date();
        const yy = String(now.getFullYear()).slice(-2);
        const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
        const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
        const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, "0");
        const min = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
        const ss = String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, "0");
        const timeStampStr = `${yy}.${mm}.${dd}_${hh}.${min}.${ss}`;

        // วนลูปสินค้าในตะกร้าเพื่อยิงแยก Record ส่งคลาวด์ลง Firestore
        for (const product of selectedProducts) {
            const currentShopId = product?.shop_id || "UNKNOWN";

            // use only 4 first chars of shop_id to make order-nr
            const shortShop = currentShopId.slice(0, 4);
            const customOrderNumber = `${shortShop}_${timeStampStr}`;
            // order_number = shop_id + timestamp e.g. t1le_26.06.06_06.55.00

            await addDoc(collection(db, COLLECTION), {
                order_number: customOrderNumber,
                [KEY.USER_ID]: buyerUid,
                [KEY.PRODUCT_ID]: product.id,
                qty: Number(product?.qty) || 0,
                shop_id: currentShopId,
                created_at: serverTimestamp(),
            });

            // 🎯 Next Step Action: Clear all products from the local basket store immediately after successful checkout
            storeSpList.getState().clearCart();
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("ERROR at create order:", error);
        throw error;
    }
}

import { create } from "zustand";

const storeOrderList = create((set, get) => {

        orders: [],

        // 💡 ฟังก์ชันสำหรับดาวน์โหลดสเตตออเดอร์จาก DB ยัดลงเครื่องคอมพิวเตอร์
        setOrders: (orders) => {
            set({ orders });

        // 💡 ฟังก์ชันล้างประวัติคำสั่งซื้อทั้งหมดในเครื่องให้โล่งสะอาดเป็นค่าว่าง
        clearOrders: () => {
            set({ orders: [] });
        },
    };

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\product\product.service.js]

import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";

import { productController } from "@/stores/store.global";

export const createProductService = () => {
    /**
     * all products from global store
     */
    const getAllProducts = () => {
        return productController.getRecords();
    };

    /**
     * find product by owner uid
     */
    const getMyProduct = (uid) => {
        const products = getAllProducts();

        return products.find((product) => product?.[KEY.USER_ID] === uid);
    };

    /**
     * find product by product id
     */
    const getProductById = (productId) => {
        const products = getAllProducts();

        return products.find((product) => product.id === productId);
    };

    return {
        getAllProducts,
        getMyProduct,
        getProductById,
    };
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.card.jsx]

import { useState } from "react";

import { L, formatCurrency, formatNumberLabel, getCurrencyLabel } from "@/i18n";

import { PAGE } from "@/config/config.app";

import Preview from "@/components/Preview";

import Modal from "@/components/Modal";

import { getAllUsersCarts } from "./record.ProductAllUsers";
