function RecordCard({
    record,
    setEditRecord,
    highlight,
    onDelete,
    loadingId,
    RECORD_FIELDS,
    isPage_Shop,
    isPage_Product,
    isPage_Select,
    isPage_Order,
    active_ShopId,
    setActive_ShopId,
    active_Order,
    setActive_Order,
    allUsersCarts = [], // ➕ ส่งตะกร้าของทุกคนลงไป
    setShowSelectProduct,
}) {
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    const user = useAuth();
    const users = storeUser((state) => state.records);

    const [previewItem, setPreviewItem] = useState(null);

    const handleDelete = async (id, name) => {
        const ok = window.confirm(`${L.delete} ${name}?`);

        if (!ok) return;

        await onDelete(id);
    };

    const labelText = (field) => {
        const value = record[field.key];

        const label = field.options?.length
            ? (field.options.find((o) => o.value === value)?.label ?? value)
            : value;

        return (
            <p className="label" key={field.key}>
                <span className="label-name">{field.label}:</span>
                <span className="label-value">{label}</span>
            </p>
        );
    };

    const labelOwner = (field) => {
        const ownerId = record?.[field.key];

        const owner = users.find((u) => u.id === ownerId);

        const ownerName = owner?.[KEY.NAME] || user?.displayName || "UNKNOWN";

        return (
            <p className="label" key={field.key}>
                <span className="label-name">{field.label}:</span>

                <span className="label-value">{ownerName}</span>
            </p>
        );
    };

    const labelNumber = (field) => {
        return (
            <p className="label" key={field.key}>
                <span className="label-name">{field.label}:</span>
                <span className="label-value">
                    {formatNumberLabel(record[field.key])} {field.unit}
                </span>
            </p>
        );
    };

    const labelCurrency = (field) => {
        return (
            <p className="label" key={field.key}>
                <span className="label-name">{field.label}:</span>
                <span className="label-value">
                    {formatCurrency(record[field.key])} {getCurrencyLabel()}
                </span>
            </p>
        );
    };

    const card_action = (
        <div className="card-action">
            <button
                className="crud-btn-edit"
                data-tooltip={L.edit}
                onClick={() => setEditRecord(record)}
            >
                <p className="icon">✒️</p>
                <p className="text">{L.edit}</p>
            </button>

            <button
                disabled={loadingId === record.id}
                className="crud-btn-delete"
                onClick={() =>
                    handleDelete(record.id, record[HEADER_FIELD?.key])
                }
            >
                {loadingId === record.id ? (
                    "..."
                ) : (
                    <>
                        <p className="icon">❌</p>
                        <p className="text">{L.delete}</p>
                    </>
                )}
            </button>
        </div>
    );

    /**
     * THE BIG GOLDEN BUTTON TO SHOW MODAL PRODUCT LIST
     * @param {*} btn_name
     * @returns
     */
    function btn_show_product(btn_name) {
        let btnClass = "btn-open-shop-product";

        if (isPage_Order) {
            btnClass = "btn-open-order-list";
        }

        if (isPage_Select) {
            btnClass = "btn-open-shop-product";
        }
        return (
            <button
                className={btnClass}
                onClick={() => {
                    if (isPage_Select) setActive_ShopId(record.id);
                    if (isPage_Order) setActive_Order(record.order_number);
                }}
            >
                <span className="icon-in-btn">📦</span>
                <span className="text-in-btn">{btn_name}</span>
            </button>
        );
    }

    /**
     * filter the RECORD_FIELDS
     */
    const HEADER_FIELD = RECORD_FIELDS.find((field) => field.IS_TITLE);
    const BODY_FIELDS = RECORD_FIELDS.filter((field) => !field.IS_TITLE);
    const BODY_FIELD_RENDERER = (field) => {
        /**
         * TEXT FIELD
         */
        if (
            [
                TYPE.TEXT,
                TYPE.EMAIL,
                TYPE.PHONE,
                TYPE.SELECT,
                TYPE.BOOLEAN,
            ].includes(field.type)
        ) {
            return labelText(field);
        }

        /**
         * NUMBER FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.NUMBER) {
            return labelNumber(field);
        }

        /**
         * CURRENCY FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.CURRENCY) {
            return labelCurrency(field);
        }

        if (
            isPage_Shop &&
            field.type === TYPE.FID &&
            field.key === KEY.USER_ID
        ) {
            return labelOwner(field);
        }

        return null;
    };
    const card_description = (
        <div className="card-description text-bg">
            {/* card has no more than 2 field to show, 
            user can see data in edit form  */}
            {BODY_FIELDS.slice(0, 2).map(BODY_FIELD_RENDERER)}
        </div>
    );
    const IMAGE_FIELDS = RECORD_FIELDS.filter(
        (field) => field.type === TYPE.IMAGE,
    );
    const hasImageConfig = RECORD_FIELDS.some(
        (field) => field.type === TYPE.IMAGE,
    );

    /**
     * in future a record can have more than 1 image,
     * we use the 1st image to show in card
     */
    const IMAGE = IMAGE_FIELDS[0]?.key;

    let OWNER = null;
    let SELECTED_COUNT = null;

    if (isPage_Product) {
        const ownerUser = users.find(
            (user) => user.id === record?.[KEY.USER_ID],
        );
        const ownerName = ownerUser?.[KEY.NAME] || "Unknown";
        const shopService = createShopService();
        const ownerShop = shopService.getShopById(record?.[KEY.SHOP_ID]);
        const shopName = ownerShop?.[KEY.NAME] || "Unknown Shop";
        const photo = ownerShop?.[KEY.IMAGE];
        OWNER = useOwnerBadge(shopName, ownerName, photo, setPreviewItem);

        /**
         * calculate all product count in all user basket
         */
        SELECTED_COUNT = getAllUsersCarts(
            allUsersCarts,
            isPage_Product,
            record,
            DEV,
        );
    }

    /**
     * ORDER PAGE
     */
    let orderTitle = record[HEADER_FIELD?.key];
    let orderImage = record[KEY.IMAGE];

    if (isPage_Order) {
        const customerData = users.find((u) => u.id === record?.user_id);

        orderImage = customerData?.[KEY.IMAGE] || customerData?.image;

        orderTitle = record?.order_number;
    }

    const RENDER_IMAGE =
        hasImageConfig && (isPage_Order ? orderImage : record[IMAGE]) ? (
            <img
                className="img-card text-bg"
                src={isPage_Order ? orderImage : record[IMAGE]}
                alt={isPage_Order ? orderTitle : record[HEADER_FIELD?.key]}
                onClick={() =>
                    setPreviewItem(isPage_Order ? orderImage : record[IMAGE])
                }
            />
        ) : (
            <div className="img-card text-bg no-image">
                <span>{L.no_image}</span>
            </div>
        );

    return (
        <div
            className={`card-container ${record.id === highlight?.id ? "highlight" : ""}`}
            id={record.id}
        >
            {active_ShopId === record.id && (
                <Modal onClose={() => setActive_ShopId(null)}>
                    <SpList
                        shop_id={record.id}
                        onClose={() => setActive_ShopId(null)}
                        setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
                    />
                </Modal>
            )}

            {active_Order === record.order_number && (
                <Modal onClose={() => setActive_Order(null)}>
                    <OrderList
                        key={record.order_number}
                        user_id={record.user_id}
                        order_number={record.order_number}
                        onClose={() => setActive_Order(null)}
                        setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
                    />
                </Modal>
            )}

            {hasImageConfig && previewItem && (
                <Preview
                    item={previewItem}
                    onClose={() => setPreviewItem(null)}
                    escapeEnabled={true}
                    escapePriority={100}
                />
            )}

            {record.id === highlight?.id && (
                <span className="badge-new">
                    {highlight?.action === "edit" ? L.update : L.new}
                </span>
            )}
            {SELECTED_COUNT}

            <div className="card-header text-bg">
                {isPage_Order
                    ? record?.order_number
                    : record[HEADER_FIELD?.key]}
                {OWNER}
            </div>
            <div className="card-body">
                {RENDER_IMAGE}

                {!isPage_Select && !isPage_Order && card_description}
                {!isPage_Select && !isPage_Order && card_action}

                {isPage_Select && btn_show_product(L.open_shop_product)}
                {isPage_Order && btn_show_product(L.open_order_product)}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

export default RecordCard;

    PHONE: "phone",

    PRICE: "price",

    STOCK: "stock",

    USER_ID: "user_id",

    SHOP_ID: "shop_id",

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.controller.jsx]

import { useEffect } from "react";

function useController(store) {
    const {
        records,
        fetch,
        add,
        update,
        remove,
        loadingId,
        recordError,
        isRecordLoading,
    } = store();

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch();
    }, [fetch]);

    return {
        records,
        loadingId,
        isRecordLoading,
        recordError,
        fetch,
        add,
        update,
        remove,
    };
}

export default useController;

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.form.fields.jsx]

import { L, formatCurrency } from "@/i18n";
import { normalizeCurrencyInput } from "@/utils/input.utils";
import { TYPE, KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";

export default function RecordFormFields(props) {
    const {
        form,
        errors,
        touched,
        handleBlur,
        inputRef,
        updateField,
        RECORD_FIELDS,
    } = props;

    const showError = (field) => {
        return (
            touched?.[field.key] &&
            errors?.[field.key] && (
                <p className="error-text">{errors?.[field.key]}</p>
            )
        );
    };

    const inputText = (field) => {
        return (
            <div key={field.key} className="input-group-text">
                <span>{field.label}</span>
                <input
                    ref={field.key === "name" ? inputRef : null}
                    className={`input ${
                        touched?.[field.key] && errors?.[field.key]
                            ? "input-error"
                            : ""
                    }`}
                    type={`${field.type}`}
                    value={form?.[field.key] ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField(field.key, e.target.value)}
                    onBlur={() => handleBlur(field.key)}
                />

                {showError(field)}
            </div>
        );
    };

    const inputEmail = (field) => {
        return (
            <div key={field.key} className="input-group-text">
                <span>{field.label}</span>

                <input
                    type={`${field.type}`}
                    className={`input ${
                        touched?.[field.key] && errors?.[field.key]
                            ? "input-error"
                            : ""
                    }`}
                    value={form[field.key]}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField(field.key, e.target.value)}
                    onBlur={() => handleBlur(field.key)}
                />

                {showError(field)}
            </div>
        );
    };

    const inputNumber = (field) => {
        return (
            <div key={field.key} className="input-group-numeric">
                <span>{field.label}</span>

                <div className="input-with-unit">
                    <input
                        type={`${field.type}`}
                        className={`input ${
                            touched?.[field.key] && errors?.[field.key]
                                ? "input-error"
                                : ""
                        }`}
                        value={form[field.key]}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField(field.key, e.target.value)}
                        onBlur={() => handleBlur(field.key)}
                    />

                    <span className="value-unit">{field.unit}</span>
                </div>

                {showError(field)}
            </div>
        );
    };

    const inputCurrency = (field) => {
        const rawValue = form[field.key] ?? "";

        const isFocused = document.activeElement?.name === field.key;

        /**
         * Currency input behavior:
         * - Empty value → show nothing
         * - While typing (focused) → show raw input
         * - After blur → show formatted currency
         */
        const displayValue = rawValue
            ? isFocused
                ? rawValue
                : formatCurrency(rawValue)
            : "";

        return (
            <div key={field.key} className="input-group-numeric">
                <span>{field.label}</span>

                <div className="input-with-unit">
                    <input
                        type="tel" //for numberic keyboard on mobile
                        inputMode="decimal"
                        name={field.key}
                        className={`input ${
                            touched?.[field.key] && errors?.[field.key]
                                ? "input-error"
                                : ""
                        }`}
                        value={displayValue}
                        onFocus={() => {
                            const cleaned = String(rawValue)
                                .replace(/,/g, "") // remove commas
                                .replace(/\.0+$/, ""); // 👈 delete .00 onFocus

                            updateField(field.key, cleaned);
                        }}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                            const result = normalizeCurrencyInput(
                                e.target.value,
                            );

                            if (result === null) return;

                            updateField(field.key, result);
                        }}
                        onBlur={() => handleBlur(field.key)}
                    />

                    <span className="value-unit">{field.unit}</span>
                </div>

                {showError(field)}
            </div>
        );
    };

    const inputSelect = (field) => {
        const currentValue = form?.[field.key] ?? field.options?.[0]?.value;

        return (
            <div key={field.key} className="input-group-text">
                <span>{field.label}</span>

                <div className="toggle-group">
                    {field.options.map((option) => {
                        const isActive = currentValue === option.value;

                        return (
                            <button
                                key={option.value}
                                type="button"
                                className={`btn-toggle ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`}
                                onClick={() =>
                                    updateField(field.key, option.value)
                                }
                                onBlur={() => handleBlur(field.key)}
                            >
                                {option.label}
                            </button>
                        );
                    })}
                </div>

                {showError(field)}
            </div>
        );
    };

    const inputBoolean = (field) => {
        const value = form?.[field.key] ?? true;

        const options = field.options ?? [
            { value: true, label: "ON" },
            { value: false, label: "OFF" },
        ];

        const current = options.find((o) => o.value === value);

        return (
            <div key={field.key} className="input-group-text">
                <span>{field.label}</span>

                <div className="switch-row">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className={`switch ${value ? "on" : "off"}`}
                        onClick={() => updateField(field.key, !value)}
                        onBlur={() => handleBlur(field.key)}
                    >
                        <div className="knob" />
                    </button>

                    <span className={`state-label ${value ? "on" : "off"}`}>
                        {current?.label ?? value}
                    </span>
                </div>

                {showError(field)}
            </div>
        );
    };

    return RECORD_FIELDS.map((field) => {
        /**
         * IMAGE FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.IMAGE) return null;

        /**
         * TEXT FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.TEXT || field.type === TYPE.PHONE) {
            return inputText(field);
        }

        /**
         * NUMBER FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.NUMBER) {
            return inputNumber(field);
        }

        /**
         * CURRENCY FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.CURRENCY) {
            return inputCurrency(field);
        }

        /**
         * EMAIL FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.EMAIL) {
            return inputEmail(field);
        }

        /**
         * SELECT FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.SELECT) {
            return inputSelect(field);
        }

        /**
         * BOOLEAN FIELD
         */
        if (field.type === TYPE.BOOLEAN) {
            return inputBoolean(field);
        }

        if (field.key === KEY.USER_ID) return null;

        return null;
    });
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.form.hook.jsx]

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";

import useController from "./record.controller";

import { ImageForm } from "@/components/ImageForm";
import RecordForm from "./record.form";
import { L } from "@/i18n";
import { validateRecord } from "@/utils/ui.validator";

function useRecordForm(
    editRecord,
    setEditRecord,
    triggerHighlight,
    RECORD_FIELDS,
    store,
    addLabel,
) {
    const controller = useController(store);

    /**
     * Build initial form state from schema + existing editRecord
     *
     * This creates the default form values when:
     * - Opening "edit mode" → use existing editRecord values
     * - Opening "create mode" → use defaultValue from schema
     *
     * Fallback order:
     * 1. editRecord value (edit mode)
     * 2. field.defaultValue (schema default)
     * 3. empty string (safe fallback for UI)
     */
    const initialForm = useMemo(() => {
        return RECORD_FIELDS.reduce((accumulator, field) => {
            accumulator[field.key] =
                editRecord?.[field.key] ?? field.defaultValue ?? "";

            return accumulator;
        }, {});
    }, [editRecord, RECORD_FIELDS]);

    const [form, setForm] = useState(initialForm);

    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});
    const [touched, setTouched] = useState({});

    const [showAddForm, setShowAddForm] = useState(false);

    const showEditForm = Boolean(editRecord);
    const showForm = showAddForm || showEditForm;

    useEffect(() => {
        setForm(initialForm);
        setErrors({});
        setTouched({});
    }, [initialForm, showAddForm]); //clear for for next add

    const hasUnsavedChanges =
        JSON.stringify(form) !== JSON.stringify(initialForm);

    /**
     * Validate Record Fields ----------------------
     */
    const updateField = (field, value) => {
        setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
    };

    const handleBlur = (field) => {
        setTouched((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: true }));

        const value = form[field];

        updateField(field, typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : value);
    };

    /**
     * Mark all form fields as "touched"
     *
     * Used when form submission fails validation.
     * This forces all validation errors to be displayed,
     * even for fields the user has not interacted with.
     *
     * Purpose:
     * - Trigger full validation UI feedback
     * - Ensure no hidden validation errors remain unseen
     */
    const markAllTouched = () =>
        RECORD_FIELDS.reduce((acc, field) => {
            acc[field.key] = true;
            return acc;
        }, {});

    /**
     * Build final payload before sending to backend
     *
     * This function transforms UI form state into a clean API-ready object.
     *
     * Responsibilities:
     * - Map form values using schema (RECORD_FIELDS)
     * - Normalize data types for backend consistency
     * - Remove UI-specific representations (e.g. string numbers)
     */
    const buildPayload = (form) =>
        RECORD_FIELDS.reduce((acc, field) => {
            const value = form[field.key];

            // convert numeric fields before save
            if (field.type === "number" || field.type === "currency") {
                acc[field.key] = Number(value);
            } else {
                acc[field.key] = value;
            }

            return acc;
        }, {});

    /**
     * Submit form flow:
     * 1. Validate input editRecord
     * 2. If errors exist → show all fields as touched and stop
     * 3. Convert form editRecord into API payload format
     * 4. Send to backend (create or update)
     */
    const submit = async (customForm = form) => {
        const newErrors = validateRecord(customForm, RECORD_FIELDS); // validate input

        setErrors(newErrors);

        if (Object.values(newErrors).some(Boolean)) {
            setTouched(markAllTouched()); // mark all invalid fields
            return false;
        }

        const payload = buildPayload(customForm); // normalize editRecord for backend

        //send to backend
        if (showEditForm) {
            await handleEdit(payload); // update existing editRecord
        } else {
            await handleAdd(payload); // create new editRecord
        }

        return true;
    };

    /**
     * handle UI -----------------------------
     */
    const handleClose = () => {
        if (hasUnsavedChanges) {
            const ok = window.confirm(L.warn_leaving);

            if (!ok) return;
        }

        setShowAddForm(false);

        if (setEditRecord) setEditRecord(null);
    };

    const handleAdd = async (data) => {
        const id = await controller.add(data);

        setShowAddForm(false);

        if (triggerHighlight) {
            triggerHighlight(id, "add");
        }
    };

    const handleEdit = async (data) => {
        if (!editRecord) return;

        await controller.update(editRecord.id, data);

        if (setEditRecord) setEditRecord(null);

        if (triggerHighlight) {
            triggerHighlight(editRecord.id, "edit");
        }
    };

    return {
        // FORM STATE
        form,
        errors,
        touched,
        hasUnsavedChanges,

        // ACTIONS
        updateField,
        handleBlur,
        submit,

        // UI CONTROL
        showForm,
        setShowAddForm,
        showEditForm,

        // HANDLER
        handleClose,

        // DATA + HELPERS
        recordCount: controller.records.length,
        image: editRecord?.image,

        // RECORD_FIELDS
        hasImageConfig: RECORD_FIELDS.some((field) => field.key === "image"),
        RECORD_FIELDS,
        addLabel,
    };
}

export default useRecordForm;

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

import { ImageForm } from "@/components/ImageForm";
import { L } from "@/i18n";
import Preview from "@/components/Preview";
import RecordFormFields from "./record.form.fields";
import { TYPE, KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";
import { createShopService } from "@/features/shop/shop.service";
import { usePermit } from "@/config/config.permit";
import { PAGE } from "@/config/config.app";
import useController from "./record.controller";
import { storeShop } from "@/stores/store.global";

function RecordForm(props) {
    const {
        image,
        handleClose,
        form,
        errors,
        touched,
        showEditForm,
        updateField,
        handleBlur,
        submit,
        RECORD_FIELDS,
        addLabel,
    } = props;
    const user = useAuth();

    /**
     * REFs
     *
     * imageRef (new_image)
     * - Used to track REPLACE image state inside ImageForm
     * - Helps verify whether a new image was selected before submit
     */
    const imageRef = useRef(null);

    /**
     * inputRef (AUTO FOCUS)
     * - attached to the "name" in FormField
     */
    const inputRef = useRef(null);

    const [previewImage, setPreviewImage] = useState(null);

    const controller = useController(storeShop);
    const { isPage_Product } = usePermit(PAGE.PRODUCT, controller);

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        let shop = null;

        if (isPage_Product) {
            const shopService = createShopService();

            shop = shopService.getMyShop(user?.uid);
        }

        const image = imageRef.current?.getResolvedImage?.();

        submit({
            ...form,

            image,

            [KEY.USER_ID]: user?.uid,

            ...(isPage_Product && {
                [KEY.SHOP_ID]: shop?.id,
            }),
        });
    };

    const hasImageConfig = RECORD_FIELDS.some(
        (field) => field.type === TYPE.IMAGE,
    );

    return (
        <form
            className={showEditForm ? "form-edit" : "form-add"}
            onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        >
            {previewImage && hasImageConfig && (
                <Preview
                    className="ifm-img"
                    item={previewImage}
                    onClose={() => setPreviewImage(null)}
                    escapePriority={100}
                />
            )}

            <div className="form-header text-bg">
                {showEditForm ? L.edit : addLabel}
            </div>

            <div className="form-body">
                {hasImageConfig && (
                    <ImageForm
                        ref={imageRef}
                        form={form}
                        image={image}
                        updateField={updateField}
                        submit={submit}
                        setPreviewImage={setPreviewImage}
                    />
                )}

                <div className="form-description text-bg">
                    <RecordFormFields
                        form={form}
                        errors={errors}
                        touched={touched}
                        handleBlur={handleBlur}
                        inputRef={inputRef}
                        updateField={updateField}
                        RECORD_FIELDS={RECORD_FIELDS}
                    />
                </div>

                <div className="form-action">
                    <button className="crud-btn-save" type="submit">
                        {L.save}
                    </button>

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="crud-btn-cancel"
                        onClick={handleClose}
                    >
                        {L.cancel}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </form>
    );
}

export default RecordForm;

import RecordCard from "@/features/record/record.card";
import LoadingView from "@/components/LoadingView";
import { usePermit } from "@/config/config.permit";
import { L } from "@/i18n";
import { TYPE, KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";
import { useDev } from "@/components/DevProvider";
import { createShopService } from "@/features/shop/shop.service";

function RecordList(props) {
    const {
        setEditRecord,
        highlight,
        RECORD_FIELDS,
        controller,
        title,
        no_record,
        PAGE_NAME,
        active_ShopId,
        setActive_ShopId,
        active_Order,
        setActive_Order,
        allUsersCarts, // ➕ ส่งตะกร้าของทุกคนลงไป
        setShowSelectProduct,
    } = props;
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    if (controller?.isRecordLoading) {
        return <LoadingView loadingName={title} />;
    }

    if (controller?.recordError) {
        return (
            <div className="card-container">
                <h1 className="text-bg">{L.no_db_connection}</h1>
                <h2 className="text-bg">{controller.recordError}</h2>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!controller?.records.length) {
        return (
            <div className="card-container">
                <h1 className="text-bg">{no_record}</h1>
            </div>
        );
    }

    const { isPage_Shop, isPage_Product, isPage_Select, isPage_Order } =
        usePermit(PAGE_NAME, controller);
    const user = useAuth();

    function renderRecordCard(record) {
        return (
            <RecordCard
                key={record.id} //React use key to find record, that user interacts with
                record={record}
                setEditRecord={setEditRecord}
                highlight={highlight}
                onDelete={controller.remove}
                loadingId={controller.loadingId}
                RECORD_FIELDS={RECORD_FIELDS}
                isPage_Shop={isPage_Shop}
                isPage_Product={isPage_Product}
                isPage_Select={isPage_Select}
                isPage_Order={isPage_Order}
                active_ShopId={active_ShopId}
                setActive_ShopId={setActive_ShopId}
                active_Order={active_Order}
                setActive_Order={setActive_Order}
                allUsersCarts={allUsersCarts} // ➕ ส่งตะกร้าของทุกคนลงไป
                setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
            />
        );
    }

    if ((isPage_Shop || isPage_Product) && !DEV) {
        return controller.records
            .filter((record) => record?.[KEY.USER_ID] === user?.uid)
            .map((record) => renderRecordCard(record));
    }

    if (isPage_Order) {
        const groupedByOrderNumber = controller.records.reduce(
            (resultGroup, currentRecord) => {
                const orderNo = currentRecord?.order_number;
                if (!orderNo) return resultGroup;

                const existingGroup = resultGroup.find(
                    (o) => o.order_number === orderNo,
                );

                if (existingGroup) {
                    existingGroup.qty =
                        (existingGroup.qty || 0) + (currentRecord?.qty || 0);
                } else {
                    resultGroup.push({
                        id: currentRecord.id,
                        order_number: orderNo,
                        user_id: currentRecord?.user_id,
                        shop_id: currentRecord?.shop_id,
                        product_id: currentRecord?.product_id,
                        qty: currentRecord?.qty || 0,
                    });
                }
                return resultGroup;
            },
            [],
        );

        /**
         * hide order of other shop owner
         */
        if (!DEV) {
            const shopService = createShopService();
            const shop = shopService.getShopByUserId(user?.uid);
            return groupedByOrderNumber
                .filter((order) => order?.shop_id === shop?.id)
                .map((order) => renderRecordCard(order));
        }

        return groupedByOrderNumber.map((order) => renderRecordCard(order));
    }

    //default = isPage_Select all customer with no login
    return controller.records.map((record) => renderRecordCard(record));
}

export default RecordList;

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.owner.jsx]

import { useState } from "react";
import { L } from "@/i18n";

export function useOwnerBadge(shopName, ownerName, photo, setPreviewItem) {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);

    // 💡 ฟังก์ชันจัดการคลิกที่ตัวกล่องแคปซูลหลัก
    const handleContainerClick = (e) => {
        // ถ้าลิ้นชักกางอยู่ แตะตรงไหนในกรอบนี้ก็นับเพื่อสั่งปิดทันที
        if (isOpen) {
            setIsOpen(false);
        }
    };

    // 💡 ฟังก์ชันเฉพาะตอนกดปุ่มเปิด/ปิด
    const handleToggleBtnClick = (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation(); // กันไม่ให้เหตุการณ์วิ่งซ้ำซ้อน
        setIsOpen(!isOpen);
    };

    return (
        <div
            // เมื่อเปิดอยู่ (isOpen) จะเข้าเงื่อนไข แตะตรงไหนก็ได้ในกรอบเพื่อปิด
            className={`card-owner ${isOpen ? "sp-owner-open" : ""}`}
            title={`${L.shop}: ${shopName}\n${L.user}: ${ownerName}`}
            onClick={handleContainerClick}
        >
            {photo && (
                <img
                    className="card-owner-img"
                    src={photo}
                    alt={shopName}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                        // 💡 สำคัญ: ถ้าลิ้นชักเปิดอยู่ ให้แตะรูปเพื่อปิดแถบแทน แต่ถ้าปิดอยู่ ให้เปิดพรีวิวภาพใหญ่ตามปกติ
                        if (isOpen) {
                            setIsOpen(false);
                        } else {
                            setPreviewItem(photo);
                        }
                    }}
                />
            )}

            {/* ปุ่มกดเปิด/ปิด ที่สลับสัญลักษณ์และมีขนาดใหญ่ขึ้น */}
            <button
                className="card-owner-toggle-btn"
                onClick={handleToggleBtnClick}
                type="button"
            >
                {isOpen ? "✕" : "ℹ️"}
            </button>

            <div className="card-owner-info">
                <p className="card-owner-shop">{shopName}</p>
                <p className="card-owner-name">{ownerName}</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.page.jsx]

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { createRecordService } from "@/services/service.record";

import RecordForm from "@/features/record/record.form";
import RecordList from "@/features/record/record.list";
import useRecordForm from "@/features/record/record.form.hook";
import useController from "./record.controller";
import "@/features/record/record.css";

import ButtonSpList from "@/features/select/btn-select-products";
import Selected from "@/features/select/selected";

import useHighlight from "@/hooks/highlight/useHighlight";
import Modal from "@/components/Modal";
import { useDev } from "@/components/DevProvider";

import { usePermit } from "@/config/config.permit";

import { KEY } from "./record.config";
import { L } from "@/i18n";

import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";

const selectProductService = createRecordService("select_products");

function RecordPage({
    setHeaderConfig,
    store,
    title,
    no_record,
    unit,
    addLabel,
    RECORD_FIELDS,
    PAGE_NAME,
}) {
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    const controller = useController(store);

    const [editRecord, setEditRecord] = useState();

    const { highlight, triggerHighlight } = useHighlight();

    const [active_ShopId, setActive_ShopId] = useState(null);

    const [active_Order, setActive_Order] = useState(null);

    const [showSelectProduct, setShowSelectProduct] = useState(false);

    const formHook = useRecordForm(
        editRecord,
        setEditRecord,
        triggerHighlight,
        RECORD_FIELDS,
        store,
        addLabel,
    );

    const { setShowAddForm, showForm, handleClose } = formHook;

    const { canAdd, isPage_Shop, isPage_Product, isPage_Select } = usePermit(
        PAGE_NAME,
        controller,
    );

    const user = useAuth();

    const userRecords =
        !DEV && (isPage_Shop || isPage_Product)
            ? controller.records.filter(
                  (record) => record?.[KEY.USER_ID] === user?.uid,
              )
            : controller.records;

    const [allUsersCarts, setAllUsersCarts] = useState([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        async function fetchAllCarts() {
            if (DEV && isPage_Product) {
                try {
                    const records = await selectProductService.getRecords();
                    const temporaryCarts = [];

                    if (Array.isArray(records)) {
                        for (const item of records) {
                            const rawCart = item?.select_product;
                            let userCart = [];

                            if (Array.isArray(rawCart)) {
                                userCart = rawCart;
                            } else if (typeof rawCart === "string") {
                                try {
                                    const parsed = JSON.parse(rawCart);
                                    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
                                        userCart = parsed;
                                    }
                                } catch {
                                    userCart = [];
                                }
                            }

                            temporaryCarts.push(...userCart);
                        }
                    }

                    setAllUsersCarts(temporaryCarts);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error(
                        "Failed to fetch all users carts for DEV:",
                        error,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        fetchAllCarts();
    }, [DEV, isPage_Product, controller.records.length]);

    useEffect(() => {
        setHeaderConfig({
            title: (
                <>
                    <div className="header-title text-bg">{title}</div>
                </>
            ),
            actions: (
                <>
                    {(canAdd || DEV) && !isPage_Select && (
                        <button
                            className="crud-btn-add"
                            onClick={() => setShowAddForm(true)}
                        >
                            <p className="icon">➕</p>
                            <p className="text">{addLabel}</p>
                        </button>
                    )}
                    {isPage_Select && (
                        <ButtonSpList
                            showSelectProduct={showSelectProduct}
                            setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
                        />
                    )}
                    {(!isPage_Shop || DEV) && (
                        <span className="count text-bg">
                            {userRecords.length} {unit}
                        </span>
                    )}
                </>
            ),
        });
    }, [userRecords.length, setHeaderConfig, canAdd]);

    return (
        <div className="page-container">
            {showForm && (
                <Modal onClose={handleClose}>
                    <RecordForm {...formHook} />
                </Modal>
            )}

            <RecordList
                highlight={highlight}
                setEditRecord={setEditRecord}
                RECORD_FIELDS={RECORD_FIELDS}
                controller={controller}
                title={title}
                no_record={no_record}
                PAGE_NAME={PAGE_NAME}
                active_ShopId={active_ShopId}
                setActive_ShopId={setActive_ShopId}
                active_Order={active_Order}
                setActive_Order={setActive_Order}
                allUsersCarts={allUsersCarts}
                setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
            />

            {showSelectProduct && (
                <Modal onClose={() => setShowSelectProduct(false)}>
                    <Selected onClose={() => setShowSelectProduct(false)} />
                </Modal>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

export default RecordPage;

function RecordPage({
    setHeaderConfig,
    store,
    title,
    no_record,
    unit,
    addLabel,
    RECORD_FIELDS,
    PAGE_NAME,
}) {
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    const controller = useController(store);

    const [editRecord, setEditRecord] = useState();

    const { highlight, triggerHighlight } = useHighlight();

    const [active_ShopId, setActive_ShopId] = useState(null);

    const [active_Order, setActive_Order] = useState(null);

    const [showSelectProduct, setShowSelectProduct] = useState(false);

    const formHook = useRecordForm(
        editRecord,
        setEditRecord,
        triggerHighlight,
        RECORD_FIELDS,
        store,
        addLabel,
    );

    const { setShowAddForm, showForm, handleClose } = formHook;

    const { canAdd, isPage_Shop, isPage_Product, isPage_Select } = usePermit(
        PAGE_NAME,
        controller,
    );

    const user = useAuth();

    const userRecords =
        !DEV && (isPage_Shop || isPage_Product)
            ? controller.records.filter(
                  (record) => record?.[KEY.USER_ID] === user?.uid,
              )
            : controller.records;

    // allUsersCarts state to show all selected product in the basket
    const [allUsersCarts, setAllUsersCarts] = useState([]); // เก็บตะกร้าของทุก

    useEffect(() => {
        async function fetchAllCarts() {
            if (DEV && isPage_Product) {
                try {
                    console.log("DEV =", DEV);
                    console.log("isPage_Product =", isPage_Product);
                    const querySnapshot = await getDocs(
                        collection(db, "select_products"),
                    );
                    const temporaryCarts = [];

                    querySnapshot.forEach((doc) => {
                        const userCart = doc.data().select_product || [];
                        temporaryCarts.push(...userCart); // นำมารวมกันเป็นอาร์เรย์ก้อนเดียวยาวๆ
                    });

                    setAllUsersCarts(temporaryCarts);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error(
                        "Failed to fetch all users carts for DEV:",
                        error,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        fetchAllCarts();
    }, [DEV, isPage_Product, controller.records.length]);

    useEffect(() => {
        setHeaderConfig({
            title: (
                <>
                    <div className="header-title text-bg">{title}</div>
                </>
            ),
            actions: (
                <>
                    {(canAdd || DEV) && !isPage_Select && (
                        <button
                            className="crud-btn-add"
                            onClick={() => setShowAddForm(true)}
                        >
                            <p className="icon">➕</p>
                            <p className="text">{addLabel}</p>
                        </button>
                    )}
                    {isPage_Select && (
                        <ButtonSpList
                            showSelectProduct={showSelectProduct}
                            setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
                        />
                    )}
                    {(!isPage_Shop || DEV) && (
                        <span className="count text-bg">
                            {userRecords.length} {unit}
                        </span>
                    )}
                </>
            ),
        });
    }, [userRecords.length, setHeaderConfig, canAdd]);

    return (
        <div className="page-container">
            {showForm && (
                <Modal onClose={handleClose}>
                    <RecordForm {...formHook} />
                </Modal>
            )}

            <RecordList
                highlight={highlight}
                setEditRecord={setEditRecord}
                RECORD_FIELDS={RECORD_FIELDS}
                controller={controller}
                title={title}
                no_record={no_record}
                PAGE_NAME={PAGE_NAME}
                active_ShopId={active_ShopId}

                active_Order={active_Order}

                setShowSelectProduct={setShowSelectProduct}
            />

                <Modal onClose={() => setShowSelectProduct(false)}>
                    <Selected onClose={() => setShowSelectProduct(false)} />

        </div>

}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\record\record.ProductAllUsers.jsx]

/**
 * calculate all product count in all user basket
 */
export function getAllUsersCarts(allUsersCarts, isPage_Product, record, DEV) {
    const totalQtyAcrossAllUsers = allUsersCarts
        .filter((item) => item.product_id === record.id)
        .reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.qty, 0);

    const SELECTED_COUNT = DEV &&
        isPage_Product &&
        totalQtyAcrossAllUsers > 0 && (
            <span
                className="sp-total-amount"
                style={{
                    position: "absolute",
                    left: "-5px",
                    top: "-5px",
                    fontSize: "12px",
                    padding: "2px 6px",
                    background: "red",
                    color: "white",
                    borderRadius: "50%",
                    zIndex: 10,
                }}
            >
                {totalQtyAcrossAllUsers}
            </span>
        );
    return SELECTED_COUNT;
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { USER_FIELDS } from "@/features/user/user.config";
import { TYPE, KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";

import { storeUser } from "@/stores/store.global";
import useController from "./record.controller";
import RecordForm from "@/features/record/record.form";
import useRecordForm from "@/features/record/record.form.hook";
import Modal from "@/components/Modal";
import { useDev } from "@/components/DevProvider";
// import { createUserService } from "@/services/service.user";// old
import { getUserProfile } from "@/features/user/user.service";

/**
 * Make POP up Edit Form for login User Profile
 */
export default function RecordProfile() {
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    const [editRecord, setEditRecord] = useState();
    const userService = createUserService();
    const controller = useController(storeUser);
    const user = useAuth();

    useEffect(() => {
        const load = async () => {
            const userRecord = DEV
                ? controller.records.find(
                      (record) => record?.[KEY.UID] === user?.uid,
                  )
                : await getUserProfile(user?.uid);
            // : await userService.getMyProfile(user?.uid); //old

            setEditRecord(userRecord);
        };

        if (user?.uid) load();
    }, [user?.uid]);

    const formHook = useRecordForm(
        editRecord,
        setEditRecord,
        null,
        USER_FIELDS,
        storeUser,
        null,
    );

    const { showForm, handleClose } = formHook;

    return (
        <>
            {showForm && (
                <Modal onClose={handleClose}>
                    <RecordForm {...formHook} />
                </Modal>
            )}
        </>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\btn-select-products.jsx]

import { storeSpList } from "@/stores/store.global";
import "./select.buttons.css";

export default function ButtonSpList({
    showSelectProduct,
    setShowSelectProduct,
}) {
    const selectProduct = storeSpList((state) => state.select_product);

    const count = selectProduct.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.qty, 0);

    const handleClick = () => {
        // 💡 เช็กว่าถ้ามีฟังก์ชันส่งลงมา ค่อยสั่งให้ทำงานสลับหน้า
        // แต่ถ้าไม่มี (เช่น ในหน้า Selected) มันจะเงียบนิ่งเฉยๆ ไม่มีแอคชั่น
        if (setShowSelectProduct) {
            setShowSelectProduct(true);
        }
    };

    return (
        <button
            className="btn-select-products btn-icon-only"
            onClick={handleClick}
        >
            🧺
            {count > 0 && <span className="sp-total-amount">{count}</span>}
        </button>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\select.card.jsx]

import { getFontsizer, isOverflow } from "@/utils/fontsizer";
import "@/utils/fontsizer.css";

import { useState } from "react";

import { L } from "@/i18n";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { useOwnerBadge } from "@/features/record/record.owner";
import { formatCurrency, getCurrencyLabel } from "@/i18n";

import "./select.card.css";
import { storeSpList } from "@/stores/store.global";
import Preview from "@/components/Preview";

import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";
import { saveSpList } from "@/features/select/select.service";
import { getUserById } from "@/features/user/user.service";

export default function ProductCard({ product, onClick, shop }) {
    const user = useAuth();

    const [previewItem, setPreviewItem] = useState(null);

    const selectProduct = storeSpList((state) => state.select_product);

    const addProduct = storeSpList((state) => state.addProduct);

    const removeProduct = storeSpList((state) => state.removeProduct);

    const item = selectProduct.find((x) => x.product_id === product.id);

    const qty = item?.qty ?? 0;

    const itemTotalPrice = (Number(product?.[KEY.PRICE]) || 0) * qty;

    const setQty = storeSpList((state) => state.setQty);

    const saveCart = async () => {
        if (!user) return;
        await saveSpList(user?.uid, storeSpList.getState().select_product);
    };

    const handleQtyChange = async (e) => {
        const value = Number(e.target.value);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return;

        setQty(product.id, Math.max(0, Math.floor(value)));

        await saveCart();
    };

    const handleQtyBlur = async (e) => {
        const value = Number(e.target.value);

        setQty(product.id, Math.max(0, Math.floor(value) || 0));
    };

    const countSize = getFontsizer(qty);

    const overflow = isOverflow(qty);

    const ownerUser = getUserById(shop.user_id);
    const ownerName = ownerUser?.[KEY.NAME] || "Unknown";
    const shopName = shop.name;
    const photo = shop.image;
    const OWNER = useOwnerBadge(shopName, ownerName, photo, setPreviewItem);

    return (
        <>
            {previewItem && (
                <Preview
                    item={previewItem}
                    onClose={() => setPreviewItem(null)}
                    escapeEnabled={true}
                    escapePriority={100}
                />
            )}
            <div
                className="sp-card-container"
                onClick={() => onClick?.(product)}
            >
                <div className="sp-card-header text-bg">
                    {product?.[KEY.NAME]}
                    {OWNER}
                </div>

                <div className="sp-card-body">
                    {product?.[KEY.IMAGE] ? (
                        <img
                            className="sp-img-card text-bg"
                            src={product[KEY.IMAGE]}
                            alt={product[KEY.NAME]}
                            onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                setPreviewItem(product[KEY.IMAGE]);
                            }}
                        />
                    ) : (
                        <div className="img-card text-bg no-image">
                            {L.no_image}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="sp-card-description text-bg">
                        <p className="label">
                            <span className="label-name">Price:</span>

                            <span className="label-value">
                                {formatCurrency(product?.[KEY.PRICE])}{" "}
                                {getCurrencyLabel()}
                            </span>
                        </p>
                        {/* total price for a product */}
                        <p className="label total-price-row">
                            <span className="label-name">{L.total}:</span>
                            <span className="label-value">
                                {formatCurrency(itemTotalPrice)}{" "}
                                {getCurrencyLabel()}
                            </span>
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="sp-card-action">
                        <button
                            className="btn-minus"
                            onClick={async () => {
                                removeProduct(product.id);
                                await saveCart();
                            }}
                        >
                            ➖
                        </button>

                        <input
                            className={`sp-input-amount ${countSize} ${
                                overflow ? "count-overflow" : ""
                            }`}
                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            min="0"
                            step="1"
                            value={qty}
                            onChange={handleQtyChange}
                            onBlur={handleQtyBlur}
                        />

                        <button
                            className="btn-plus"
                            onClick={async () => {
                                addProduct(product.id);
                                await saveCart();
                            }}
                        >
                            ➕
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\select.list.css]

/* ========================================================

    overflow-y: auto; /* 💡 สำคัญ: ย้าย scroll containment มาไว้ที่นี่ เหมือน .page-area ของแอปหลัก */
    min-height: 0; /* ป้องกัน layout พังใน flexbox */

.sp-header-shop-name {
    font-size: x-large;
    font-weight: bold;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\select.list.jsx]

import { useEffect } from "react";

import { useAuth } from "@/services/service.auth";

import { productController, shopController } from "@/stores/store.global";

import { L, getCurrencyLabel, formatCurrency } from "@/i18n";

import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { loadSpList } from "@/features/select/select.service";
import ProductCard from "@/features/select/select.card";
import storeSpList from "@/features/select/select.store";
import ButtonSpList from "@/features/select/btn-select-products";
import "./select.list.css";

export default function SpList({ shop_id, onClose, setShowSelectProduct }) {
    const products = productController.getRecords();

    const shop = shopController.getById(shop_id);

    const SpLists = products.filter(
        (product) => product?.[KEY.SHOP_ID] === shop_id,
    );

    const user = useAuth();

    const setSpList = storeSpList((s) => s.setSpList);

    /**
     * load products from db and put them into the basket
     * if user logged in (also DEV can see getAllUsersCarts if logged in)
     */
    useEffect(() => {
        async function initCart() {
            if (!user?.uid) return;
            const list = await loadSpList(user?.uid);
            setSpList(list);
        }

        initCart();
    }, [user?.uid]);

    const selectProduct = storeSpList((s) => s.select_product); // ดึงสเตตสินค้าในตะกร้าปัจจุบัน

    const shopTotalAmount = SpLists.reduce((totalSum, product) => {
        const cartItem = selectProduct.find(
            (item) => item.product_id === product.id,
        );
        const qty = cartItem?.qty ?? 0;
        const price = Number(product?.[KEY.PRICE]) || 0;
        return totalSum + price * qty;
    }, 0);

    /**
     * Total price of selected products in a list
     */
    const TOTAL_AMOUNT = shopTotalAmount > 0 && (
        <span className="sp-total-amount-product">
            {formatCurrency(shopTotalAmount)} {getCurrencyLabel()}
        </span>
    );

    const handleSelected = () => {
        if (setShowSelectProduct) {
            onClose(); // 1. close the activ shop product list
            setShowSelectProduct(true); // 2. open the selected products of login user
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="sp-shell">
            <div className="sp-header">
                <ButtonSpList setShowSelectProduct={handleSelected} />
                <div className="prod-count-total-price">
                    <span className="sp-header-shop-name text-bg">
                        {shop?.name}
                    </span>
                    {TOTAL_AMOUNT}
                </div>
                <span className="sp-count text-bg">
                    {SpLists.length} {L.product}
                </span>
                <button className="btn-modal-close" onClick={onClose}>
                    ✕
                </button>
            </div>

            <div className="sp-page-area">
                {SpLists.map((product) => (
                    <ProductCard
                        key={product.id}
                        product={product}
                        shop={shop}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\select.service.jsx]

import { createRecordService } from "@/services/service.record";

const selectProductService = createRecordService("select_products");

export async function loadSpList(uid) {
    if (!uid) return [];

    try {
        const records = await selectProductService.getRecords({
            user_id: uid,
        });

        const matched = Array.isArray(records)
            ? records.find(
                  (item) =>
                      item.user_id === uid ||
                      item.uid === uid ||
                      item.id === uid,
              )
            : null;

        if (!matched) return [];

        if (Array.isArray(matched.select_product)) {
            return matched.select_product;
        }

        if (typeof matched.select_product === "string") {
            try {
                const parsed = JSON.parse(matched.select_product);
                return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
            } catch {
                return [];
            }
        }

        return [];
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error loading select_products:", error);
        return [];
    }
}

export async function saveSpList(uid, spList) {
    if (!uid) return;

    try {
        const safeList = Array.isArray(spList) ? spList : [];
        const records = await selectProductService.getRecords({
            user_id: uid,
        });

        const existing = Array.isArray(records)
            ? records.find(
                  (item) =>
                      item.user_id === uid ||
                      item.uid === uid ||
                      item.id === uid,
              )
            : null;

        if (existing?.id) {
            await selectProductService.updateRecord(existing.id, {
                user_id: uid,
                select_product: safeList,
            });
        } else {
            await selectProductService.addRecord({
                user_id: uid,
                select_product: safeList,
            });
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error saving select_products:", error);
        throw error;
    }
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\select.service_old.jsx]

import { doc, getDoc, setDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "@/config/config.firebase";

const COLLECTION = "select_products";

export async function loadSpList(user_id) {
    if (!user_id) return [];
    const ref = doc(db, COLLECTION, user_id);

    const snap = await getDoc(ref);

    if (!snap.exists()) {
        return [];
    }

    return snap.data().select_product || [];
}

export async function saveSpList(user_id, select_product) {
    if (!user_id) return [];
    const ref = doc(db, COLLECTION, user_id);

    await setDoc(
        ref,
        {
            select_product,
        },
        {
            merge: true,
        },
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\select.store.jsx]

import { create } from "zustand";

const COLLECTION = "select_products";

const storeSpList = create((set, get) => {
    return {
        select_product: [],

        /**
         * user push + button on a product card in the modal select.product
         */
        addProduct: (product_id) => {
            set((state) => {
                const item = state.select_product.find(
                    (item) => item.product_id === product_id,
                );

                if (item) {
                    // case product already exist in the basket
                    const updated = state.select_product.map((item) =>
                        item.product_id === product_id
                            ? { ...item, qty: item.qty + 1 }
                            : item,
                    );

                    return { select_product: updated };
                }

                const updated = [
                    // case product not exist
                    ...state.select_product,
                    { product_id, qty: 1 }, // then add new
                ];

                return { select_product: updated };
            });
        },

        setSpList: (select_product) => {
            set({ select_product });
        },

        /**
         * when user change quantity direct in the <input />
         */
        setQty: (productId, qty) => {
            const result =
                qty <= 0 //if quantity = 0
                    ? get().select_product.filter(
                          (item) => item.product_id !== productId, // then remove from basket
                      )
                    : get().select_product.some(
                            // some = find and return true/false
                            (item) => item.product_id === productId, // if quantity > 0
                        )
                      ? get().select_product.map((item) =>
                            item.product_id === productId
                                ? { ...item, qty } // then update quantity of the product
                                : item,
                        )
                      : [
                            //if product not found in the basket
                            ...get().select_product,
                            {
                                product_id: productId, //then add product into the basket
                                qty,
                            },
                        ];

            set({ select_product: result });
        },

        /**
         * user push - button on a product card in the modal select.product
         */
        removeProduct: (product_id) => {
            const result = get()
                .select_product.map((item) =>
                    item.product_id === product_id
                        ? { ...item, qty: item.qty - 1 } //minus button click once , quantity - 1
                        : item,
                )
                .filter((item) => item.qty > 0); // and return only product which quantity > 0

            set({ select_product: result });
        },

        clearCart: () => {
            set({ select_product: [] });
        },
    };
});

export default storeSpList;



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\select\selected.jsx]

import storeSpList from "@/features/select/select.store";
import { productController } from "@/stores/store.global";
import ProductCard from "@/features/select/select.card";
import { L, getCurrencyLabel, formatCurrency } from "@/i18n";

import ButtonSpList from "@/features/select/btn-select-products";
import ButtonOrder from "@/features/order/btn-order";

import { createShopService } from "@/features/shop/shop.service";
import { getUserById } from "@/features/user/user.service";

export default function Selected({ onClose }) {
    const allProducts = productController.getRecords();

    // get all products from Zustand Store
    const spItems = storeSpList((state) => state.select_product);

    // filtered by product_id in store
    const selectedProducts = spItems
        .map((spItem) => {
            const productDetail = allProducts.find(
                (p) => p.id === spItem.product_id,
            );
            return {
                ...productDetail, // name, price, image
                qty: spItem.qty,
            };
        })
        .filter((p) => p.id); // handle filter in case no found (e.g. deleted), prevent crash undefined

    const shopService = createShopService();

    const grandTotalAmount = selectedProducts.reduce((totalSum, product) => {
        const qty = product?.qty ?? 0;
        const price = Number(product?.price) || 0;
        return totalSum + price * qty;
    }, 0);

    const TOTAL_AMOUNT = grandTotalAmount > 0 && (
        <span className="sp-total-amount-product">
            {formatCurrency(grandTotalAmount)} {getCurrencyLabel()}
        </span>
    );

    return (
        <div className="sp-shell">
            <div className="sp-header">
                {/* ButtonSpList only for show total selected products count , no action*/}
                <ButtonSpList />
                <div className="prod-count-total-price">
                    <span className="sp-count text-bg">
                        {selectedProducts.length} {L.product}
                    </span>
                    {TOTAL_AMOUNT}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-row">
                    {selectedProducts.length && <ButtonOrder />}
                    <button className="btn-modal-close" onClick={onClose}>
                        ✕
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="sp-page-area">
                {selectedProducts.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="text-bg">{L.no_product}</div>
                ) : (
                    selectedProducts.map((product) => {
                        // 💡 1. เรียกดูข้อมูลร้านค้าจาก Store กลาง (Public Data) ตรงๆ ผ่าน shop_id ของสินค้าก่อนเลย
                        // วิธีนี้ทำให้ Guest (คนไม่ล็อกอิน) สามารถดึงชื่อร้านและรูปภาพจริงของร้านมาโชว์ได้ทันที โดยแอปไม่พัง
                        let shop = product?.shop_id
                            ? shopService.getShopById(product.shop_id)
                            : null;

                        // 💡 2. ตรวจสอบเงื่อนไขตามที่พี่วางกติกาไว้: หากวิธีแรกหาไม่เจอ หรือกรณีผู้ใช้ Login แล้ว
                        // ระบบจะขยับไปดึงข้อมูลเจ้าของร้านผ่าน getUserById มาประกอบร่างเป็น Fallback ให้แม่นยำขึ้น
                        if (!shop) {
                            const ownerUser = product?.user_id
                                ? getUserById(product?.user_id)
                                : null;
                            shop = ownerUser?.uid
                                ? shopService.getMyShop(ownerUser.uid)
                                : null;
                        }

                        return (
                            <ProductCard
                                key={product.id}
                                product={product} // ส่งชื่อ ราคา รูปสินค้า
                                shop={shop} // ส่งข้อมูลร้านค้า (ที่ดึงมาจากฐานข้อมูล Public สำเร็จแล้ว)
                            />
                        );
                    })
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\shop\shop.service.js]

import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";

import { shopController } from "@/stores/store.global";

export const createShopService = () => {
    /**
     * all shops from global store
     */
    const getAllShops = () => {
        return shopController.getRecords();
    };

    /**
     * find shop by owner uid
     */
    const getMyShop = (uid) => {
        const shops = getAllShops();

        return shops.find((shop) => shop?.[KEY.USER_ID] === uid);
    };

    /**
     * find shop by shop id
     */
    const getShopById = (shopId) => {
        const shops = getAllShops();

        return shops.find((shop) => shop.id === shopId);
    };

    /**
     * find shop by user id
     */
    const getShopByUserId = (userId) => {
        const shops = getAllShops();

        return shops.find((shop) => shop.user_id === userId);
    };

    return {
        getAllShops,
        getMyShop,
        getShopById,
        getShopByUserId,
    };
};

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\user\user.service.js]

import { createRecordService } from "@/services/service.record";
import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";
import { userController } from "@/stores/store.global";

const userService = createRecordService("users");

export const getUserProfile = async (uid) => {
    if (!uid) return null;

    const users = await userService.getRecords();
    if (!Array.isArray(users)) return null;

    return (
        users.find(
            (u) =>
                String(u.uid) === String(uid) || String(u.id) === String(uid),
        ) || null
    );
};

export const getAllUsers = () => {
    return userController.getRecords();
};

export const getUserById = (userId) => {
    const users = getAllUsers();
    if (!Array.isArray(users)) return undefined;

    return users.find((user) => String(user.id) === String(userId));
};

export const syncUser = async (user) => {
    try {
        if (!user) {
            return;
        }

        const payload = {
            uid: user.uid,
            [KEY.NAME]: user.displayName || "",
            [KEY.EMAIL]: user.email || "",
            [KEY.PHONE]: user.phoneNumber || "",
            [KEY.IMAGE]: user.photoURL || "",
            [KEY.IS_ACTIVE]: true,
        };

        const existing = await getUserProfile(user.uid);

        if (existing?.id) {
            await userService.updateRecord(existing.id, payload);
        } else {
            await userService.addRecord(payload);
        }

        console.log("USER SYNCED");
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("SYNC USER ERROR");
        console.error(error);
        console.error(error.code);
        console.error(error.message);
    }
};

export const loadCurrentUserDocument = async (uid) => {
    if (!uid) return null;

    const profile = await getUserProfile(uid);
    if (!profile) return null;

    return {
        uid: profile.uid || uid,
        ...profile,
    };
};

export const deleteUserProfile = async (uid) => {
    if (!uid) return;

    const confirmDelete = window.confirm(L.warn_deleting);
    if (!confirmDelete) return;

    const target = await getUserProfile(uid);
    if (target?.id) {
        await userService.deleteRecord(target.id);
    }

    await signOut(auth);
    window.location.href = "/";
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\features\user\user.service.old.firestore.js]

import { doc, getDoc, setDoc } from "firebase/firestore";

import { db } from "@/config/config.firebase";

import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";

import { userController } from "@/stores/store.global";

export const getUserProfile = async (uid) => {
    if (!uid) return null;

    const ref = doc(db, "users", uid);
    const snap = await getDoc(ref);

    if (!snap.exists()) return null;

    return {
        id: snap.id,
        ...snap.data(),
    };
};

/**
 * all shops from global store
 */
export const getAllUsers = () => {
    return userController.getRecords();
};

/**
 * find shop by shop id
 */
export const getUserById = (userId) => {
    const users = getAllUsers();

    return users.find((user) => user.id === userId);
};

export const syncUser = async (user) => {
    try {
        if (!user) {
            return;
        }

        const userRef = doc(db, "users", user.uid);

        const payload = {
            uid: user.uid,

            [KEY.NAME]: user.displayName || "",

            [KEY.EMAIL]: user.email || "",

            [KEY.PHONE]: user.phoneNumber || "",

            [KEY.IMAGE]: user.photoURL || "",

            [KEY.IS_ACTIVE]: true,

            created_at: Date.now(),

            updatedAt: Date.now(),
        };

        await setDoc(userRef, payload);

        console.log("🔥 USER CREATED");
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("🚨 SYNC USER ERROR");
        console.error(error);
        console.error(error.code);
        console.error(error.message);
    }
};

export const loadCurrentUserDocument = async (uid) => {
    const ref = doc(db, "users", uid);
    const snap = await getDoc(ref);

    if (!snap.exists()) return null;

    return {
        uid,
        ...snap.data(),
    };
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\flows\login\google\google.login.js]

import {
    GoogleAuthProvider,
    signInWithPopup,
    signOut,
    browserLocalPersistence,
    setPersistence,
    onAuthStateChanged,
} from "firebase/auth";

import { syncUser } from "./google.user";
import { auth } from "@/config/config.firebase";
import { loadSpList } from "@/features/select/select.service";
import storeSpList from "@/features/select/select.store";

const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();

export const loginWithGoogle = async () => {
    await setPersistence(auth, browserLocalPersistence);

    return signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
};

export const logout = () => signOut(auth);

/**
 * we don't use auth listener now, but can be usefull in future
 * onAuthStateChanged
 * @param {*} auth usecase e.g. auth.currentUser
 * auth changed e.g. when:
 * login
 * logout
 * token refresh
 * session restore
 * @return unsubscribe() if call it = stop listening , but not logout
 * e.g. if App need to break some how
 * , no use for now
 */
export function startAuthListener({ setUser }) {
    return onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (firebaseUser) => {
        setUser(firebaseUser);

        if (!firebaseUser) return;

        await syncUser(firebaseUser);

        const selectProduct = await loadSpList(firebaseUser.uid);

        storeSpList.getState().setSpList(selectProduct);
    });
}

import { doc, getDoc, setDoc } from "firebase/firestore";

import { db } from "@/config/config.firebase";

import { KEY } from "@/features/record/record.config";

export const getUserProfile = async (uid) => {
    if (!uid) return null;

    const ref = doc(db, "users", uid);
    const snap = await getDoc(ref);

    if (!snap.exists()) return null;

    return {
        id: snap.id,
        ...snap.data(),
    };
};

export const syncUser = async (user) => {
    if (!user) return;

    const userRef = doc(db, "users", user.uid);

    const userSnap = await getDoc(userRef);

    // already exists
    if (userSnap.exists()) {
        return;
    }

    await setDoc(userRef, {
        uid: user.uid,

        [KEY.NAME]: user.displayName || "",

        [KEY.EMAIL]: user.email || "",

        [KEY.PHONE]: user.phoneNumber || "",

        [KEY.IMAGE]: user.photoURL || "",

        [KEY.IS_ACTIVE]: true,

        created_at: Date.now(),

        updatedAt: Date.now(),
    });
};

export const loadCurrentUserDocument = async (uid) => {
    const ref = doc(db, "users", uid);
    const snap = await getDoc(ref);

    if (!snap.exists()) return null;

    return {
        uid,
        ...snap.data(),
    };
};

import { loginWithGoogle } from "@/flows/login/google/google.login";

import "./LoginPageGoogle.css";

export default function LoginPageGoogle() {
    const language = navigator.language.toLowerCase();

    const isThai = language.startsWith("th");
    const isGerman = language.startsWith("de");

    const content = isThai
        ? {
              appName: "ระบบขายสินค้าออนไลน์",
              title: "ยินดีต้อนรับสู่",
              description:
                  "หากคุณล็อกอิน Google อยู่แล้ว\nกดปุ่มด้านล่างเพื่อเข้าสู่ระบบ",
              button: "เข้าสู่ระบบด้วย Google",
          }
        : isGerman
          ? {
                appName: "Ecommerce App",
                title: "Willkommen bei",
                description:
                    "Wenn Sie bereits bei Google angemeldet sind,\nklicken Sie unten zum Fortfahren",
                button: "Mit Google fortfahren",
            }
          : {
                appName: "Ecommerce App",
                title: "Welcome to",
                description:
                    "If you are already signed in with Google,\npress the button below to continue.",
                button: "Continue with Google",
            };

    return (
        <div className="login-page">
            <div className="login-card">
                <h1 className="login-title">
                    <span className="login-title-small">{content.title}</span>

                    <span className="login-title-main">{content.appName}</span>
                </h1>

                <p className="login-description">{content.description}</p>

                <button className="login-google-btn" onClick={loginWithGoogle}>
                    {content.button}
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
