[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\hooks\highlight\useHighlight.js]

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import "@/hooks/highlight/useHighlight.css";

/**
 * Focus user attention on a single entity card.
 *
 * Side effects:
 * - scroll into view
 * - temporarily lock page scroll
 * - apply highlight animation
 *
 * DO NOT:
 * - fetch data here
 * - mutate global state
 */

export default function useHighlight() {
    const [highlight, setHighlight] = useState(null);

    const triggerHighlight = (id, action) => {
        setHighlight({ id, action });
    };

    const clearHighlight = () => {
        setHighlight(null);
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!highlight) return;

        /**
         * delay auto scroll for 200ms,
         * wait for DOM finish rendering first
         */
        const scrollTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            const el = document.getElementById(highlight.id);

            if (el) {
                el.scrollIntoView({
                    //auto scroll
                    behavior: "smooth",
                    block: "center",
                });
            }
        }, 200);

        const clearTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            setHighlight(null);
        }, 10000); // 10s for showing highlight

        return () => {
            clearTimeout(scrollTimer);
            clearTimeout(clearTimer);
        };
    }, [highlight]);

    return {
        highlight,
        triggerHighlight,
        clearHighlight, //for e.g. future, when new order still not processed, then stay new
    };
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\hooks\hoc.withEscView.jsx]

import useKeyEscape from "@/hooks/useKeyEscape";

/**
 * Higher-Order Component (HOC)
 *
 * Adds Escape key close behavior to any wrapped component.
 *
 * This HOC is reusable across:
 * - modal components
 * - preview overlays
 * - full-screen views
 *
 * It does NOT assume UI type (modal/image/etc),
 * only behavior: "ESC triggers onClose".
 *
 * @param {React.ComponentType<any>} WrappedComponent
 * A React component that will receive all original props
 * and be enhanced with ESC-to-close behavior.
 *
 * @returns {React.ComponentType<any>}
 * A new component that wraps the original one and injects ESC behavior.
 */
export function withEscView(WrappedComponent) {
    return function Wrapped(props) {
        const { onClose, escapeEnabled = true, escapePriority = 100 } = props;

        /**
         * ESC handler wrapper
         *
         * Purpose:
         * - Normalize close behavior from props
         * - Ensure ESC system always receives a boolean "consumed" signal
         *
         * @returns {boolean}
         * true  = ESC is consumed (stop propagation)
         * false = no action taken
         */
        const handleClose = () => {
            if (typeof onClose === "function") {
                onClose();
                return true; // ESC consumed
            }

            return false;
        };

        /**
         * Register ESC handler into global ESC manager
         */
        useKeyEscape(handleClose, escapeEnabled, escapePriority);

        return <WrappedComponent {...props} />;
    };
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\hooks\useAppBootstrap.jsx]

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
    injectCss,
    removeCss,
    initCssInjectorObserver,
} from "@/utils/CssInjector/CssInjector";

import { storeProduct, storeShop, storeUser } from "@/stores/store.global";

export default function useAppBootstrap() {
    /**
     * global app theme
     */
    const [theme, setTheme] = useState(
        () => localStorage.getItem("theme") || "luxury",
    );

    /**
     * init global systems once
     */
    useEffect(() => {
        initCssInjectorObserver();

        storeUser.getState().fetch();
        storeShop.getState().fetch();
        storeProduct.getState().fetch();
    }, []);

    /**
     * sync app theme
     */
    useEffect(() => {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);

        localStorage.setItem("theme", theme);

        /**
         * inject utility css
         * only for themes that use image backgrounds
         */
        if (theme === "luxury") {
            injectCss("bg-image");
        } else {
            removeCss("bg-image");
        }
    }, [theme]);

    return {
        theme,
        setTheme,
    };
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\hooks\useAuth.js]

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { auth } from "@/firebase";
import { onAuthStateChanged } from "firebase/auth";

export const useAuth = () => {
    const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        const unsub = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            setCurrentUser(user);
            setLoading(false);
        });

        return () => unsub();
    }, []);

    return {
        currentUser,
        isLogin: !!currentUser,
        loading,
    };
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\hooks\useKeyEscape.js]

import { useEffect } from "react";

/**
 * Global registry of all Escape key handlers
 * Each component using useKeyEscape will register itself here
 */
const handlers = new Set();

/**
 * Currently active handler (top-most / last mounted layer)
 * This ensures only the active UI layer responds to ESC
 */
let activeHandler = null;

/**
 * Global Escape key listener
 * Runs once for the entire application lifecycle
 */
const handleKey = (e) => {
    if (e.key !== "Escape") return;

    /**
     * Sort handlers by priority (higher priority first)
     * This acts as a fallback system if activeHandler is missing
     */
    const sortedHandlers = [...handlers].sort(
        (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority,
    );

    /**
     * Prefer active handler (top-most UI layer)
     * Fallback to highest priority handler if active is not available
     */
    const handler = activeHandler || sortedHandlers[0];

    if (!handler) return;

    /**
     * Execute Escape handler for the active layer
     */
    const result = handler.callback();

    /**
     * Optional control:
     * If callback returns false, allow future fallback logic
     */
    if (result === false) {
        return;
    }
};

/**
 * Register global key listener once
 * This ensures ESC is handled at application level
 */
window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKey);

/**
 * Hook: useKeyEscape
 *
 * Registers a component as an ESC target
 *
 * @param {Function} callback - function to execute on ESC
 * @param {boolean} enabled - enable/disable ESC handling
 * @param {number} priority - fallback priority when activeHandler is missing
 */
export default function useKeyEscape(callback, enabled = true, priority = 0) {
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!enabled) return;

        /**
         * Create handler object for this component instance
         */
        const handler = {
            callback,
            priority,
        };

        /**
         * Register handler into global ESC registry
         */
        handlers.add(handler);

        /**
         * Mark this handler as the active (top-most) layer
         * This ensures ESC only closes the currently focused UI layer
         */
        activeHandler = handler;

        return () => {
            /**
             * Remove handler when component unmounts or disables ESC
             */
            handlers.delete(handler);

            /**
             * Clean up active reference if this was the active layer
             */
            if (activeHandler === handler) {
                activeHandler = null;
            }
        };
    }, [callback, enabled, priority]);
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\i18n\index.js]

import { LANG, BaseLang } from "./lang";

// --- normalize ---
const normalizeLang = (locale) => {
    if (!locale) return BaseLang;
    return locale.toLowerCase().split("-")[0];
};

// --- detect lang en de th ---
export const getUserLang = () => {
    const dbLang = window.__USER_LANG__;
    const browserLang = navigator.language;

    const detected = normalizeLang(dbLang || browserLang);

    // support specific language
    const supported = ["th", "de", "en"];

    if (supported.includes(detected)) {
        return detected;
    }

    // 👉 fallback
    return "en";
};

const appLang = getUserLang();

// --- validate ---
function validateLang(base, target, langName) {
    if (typeof base !== "object" || typeof target !== "object") return;

    Object.keys(base).forEach((key) => {
        // ignore metadata (ถ้ามี)
        if (key === "key") return;

        if (!(key in target)) {
            console.warn(`[i18n] Missing key "${key}" in ${langName}`);
        }
    });

    // (optional) check key เกิน
    Object.keys(target).forEach((key) => {
        if (key === "key") return;

        if (!(key in base)) {
            console.warn(`[i18n] Extra key "${key}" in ${langName}`);
        }
    });
}

// Make currency readable for no Thai people
export function getCurrencyLabel() {
    if (false === ["th"].includes(appLang)) {
        return BaseLang === "th" ? "THB" : "CHF";
    }
    return LANG[BaseLang].currency;
}

// validate lang.js
/**
 * import.meta.env.DEV = vite running in DEV mode
 *
 * e.g.
 * npm run dev
 * → true
 * npm run build
 * → false
 */
if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    const base = LANG[BaseLang];
    Object.entries(LANG).forEach(([langName, lang]) => {
        validateLang(base, lang, langName);
    });
}

// --- translator ---
export const L = new Proxy(LANG[appLang], {
    get(target, prop) {
        if (!(prop in target)) {
            console.warn(`[i18n] Missing key: ${String(prop)}`);
            return prop;
        }
        return target[prop];
    },
});

// --- currency ---
const CURRENCY_MAP = {
    th: { locale: "th-TH", currency: "THB" },
    de: { locale: "de-CH", currency: "CHF" },
    en: { locale: "en-US", currency: "CHF" }, // business rule
};

/**
 * show lang format of browser setting first or en lang
 */
export const cfg = CURRENCY_MAP[appLang] || CURRENCY_MAP.en;
const isSwiss = cfg.locale === "de-CH";

export const formatCurrency = (value) => {
    let result = new Intl.NumberFormat(cfg.locale, {
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    }).format(value);

    // Swiss override
    if (isSwiss) {
        result = result.replace(".", ",");
    }

    return result;
};

export const formatNumberLabel = (value) =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat(cfg.locale, {
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
        maximumFractionDigits: 20,
    }).format(value);

import ApplicationLogo from "@/Components/ApplicationLogo_TEST";
import Dropdown from "@/Components/Dropdown";
import NavLink from "@/Components/NavLink";
import ResponsiveNavLink from "@/Components/ResponsiveNavLink";
import { Link, usePage } from "@inertiajs/react";
import { useState } from "react";

export default function AuthenticatedLayout({ header, children }) {
    const user = usePage().props.auth.user;

    const [showingNavigationDropdown, setShowingNavigationDropdown] =
        useState(false);

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100">
            <nav className="border-b border-gray-100 bg-white">
                <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
                    <div className="flex h-16 justify-between">
                        <div className="flex">
                            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center">
                                <Link href="/">
                                    <ApplicationLogo className="block h-9 w-auto fill-current text-gray-800" />
                                </Link>
                            </div>

                            <div className="hidden space-x-8 sm:-my-px sm:ms-10 sm:flex">
                                <NavLink
                                    href={route("dashboard")}
                                    active={route().current("dashboard")}
                                >
                                    Dashboard
                                </NavLink>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="hidden sm:ms-6 sm:flex sm:items-center">
                            <div className="relative ms-3">
                                <Dropdown>
                                    <Dropdown.Trigger>
                                        <span className="inline-flex rounded-md">
                                            <button
                                                type="button"
                                                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-md border border-transparent bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium leading-4 text-gray-500 transition duration-150 ease-in-out hover:text-gray-700 focus:outline-none"
                                            >
                                                {user.name}

                                                <svg
                                                    className="-me-0.5 ms-2 h-4 w-4"
                                                    xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                                                    viewBox="0 0 20 20"
                                                    fill="currentColor"
                                                >
                                                    <path
                                                        fillRule="evenodd"
                                                        d="M5.293 7.293a1 1 0 011.414 0L10 10.586l3.293-3.293a1 1 0 111.414 1.414l-4 4a1 1 0 01-1.414 0l-4-4a1 1 0 010-1.414z"
                                                        clipRule="evenodd"
                                                    />
                                                </svg>
                                            </button>
                                        </span>
                                    </Dropdown.Trigger>

                                    <Dropdown.Content>
                                        <Dropdown.Link
                                            href={route("profile.edit")}
                                        >
                                            Profile
                                        </Dropdown.Link>
                                        <Dropdown.Link
                                            href={route("logout")}
                                            method="post"
                                            as="button"
                                        >
                                            Log Out
                                        </Dropdown.Link>
                                    </Dropdown.Content>
                                </Dropdown>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="-me-2 flex items-center sm:hidden">
                            <button
                                onClick={() =>
                                    setShowingNavigationDropdown(
                                        (previousState) => !previousState,
                                    )
                                }
                                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md p-2 text-gray-400 transition duration-150 ease-in-out hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-500 focus:bg-gray-100 focus:text-gray-500 focus:outline-none"
                            >
                                <svg
                                    className="h-6 w-6"
                                    stroke="currentColor"
                                    fill="none"
                                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                                >
                                    <path
                                        className={
                                            !showingNavigationDropdown
                                                ? "inline-flex"
                                                : "hidden"
                                        }
                                        strokeLinecap="round"
                                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                                        strokeWidth="2"
                                        d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16"
                                    />
                                    <path
                                        className={
                                            showingNavigationDropdown
                                                ? "inline-flex"
                                                : "hidden"
                                        }
                                        strokeLinecap="round"
                                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                                        strokeWidth="2"
                                        d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"
                                    />
                                </svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div
                    className={
                        (showingNavigationDropdown ? "block" : "hidden") +
                        " sm:hidden"
                    }
                >
                    <div className="space-y-1 pb-3 pt-2">
                        <ResponsiveNavLink
                            href={route("dashboard")}
                            active={route().current("dashboard")}
                        >
                            Dashboard
                        </ResponsiveNavLink>
                    </div>

                    <div className="border-t border-gray-200 pb-1 pt-4">
                        <div className="px-4">

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Auth\ConfirmPassword.jsx]

import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import GuestLayout from '@/Layouts/GuestLayout';
import { Head, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function ConfirmPassword() {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors, reset } = useForm({
        password: '',
    });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        post(route('password.confirm'), {
            onFinish: () => reset('password'),
        });
    };

    return (
        <GuestLayout>
            <Head title="Confirm Password" />

            <div className="mb-4 text-sm text-gray-600">
                This is a secure area of the application. Please confirm your
                password before continuing.
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={submit}>
                <div className="mt-4">
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="password" value="Password" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password"
                        type="password"
                        name="password"
                        value={data.password}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        isFocused={true}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('password', e.target.value)}
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-end">
                    <PrimaryButton className="ms-4" disabled={processing}>
                        Confirm
                    </PrimaryButton>
                </div>
            </form>
        </GuestLayout>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Auth\ForgotPassword.jsx]

import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import GuestLayout from '@/Layouts/GuestLayout';
import { Head, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function ForgotPassword({ status }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        email: '',
    });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        post(route('password.email'));
    };

    return (
        <GuestLayout>
            <Head title="Forgot Password" />

            <div className="mb-4 text-sm text-gray-600">
                Forgot your password? No problem. Just let us know your email
                address and we will email you a password reset link that will
                allow you to choose a new one.
            </div>

            {status && (
                <div className="mb-4 text-sm font-medium text-green-600">
                    {status}
                </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={submit}>
                <TextInput
                    id="email"
                    type="email"
                    name="email"
                    value={data.email}
                    className="mt-1 block w-full"
                    isFocused={true}
                    onChange={(e) => setData('email', e.target.value)}
                />

                <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-end">
                    <PrimaryButton className="ms-4" disabled={processing}>
                        Email Password Reset Link
                    </PrimaryButton>
                </div>
            </form>
        </GuestLayout>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Auth\Login.jsx]

import Checkbox from '@/Components/Checkbox';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import GuestLayout from '@/Layouts/GuestLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function Login({ status, canResetPassword }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors, reset } = useForm({
        email: '',
        password: '',
        remember: false,
    });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        post(route('login'), {
            onFinish: () => reset('password'),
        });
    };

    return (
        <GuestLayout>
            <Head title="Log in" />

            {status && (
                <div className="mb-4 text-sm font-medium text-green-600">
                    {status}
                </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={submit}>
                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="email" value="Email" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="email"
                        type="email"
                        name="email"
                        value={data.email}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="username"
                        isFocused={true}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('email', e.target.value)}
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4">
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="password" value="Password" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password"
                        type="password"
                        name="password"
                        value={data.password}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="current-password"
                        onChange={(e) => setData('password', e.target.value)}
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 block">
                    <label className="flex items-center">
                        <Checkbox
                            name="remember"
                            checked={data.remember}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                                setData('remember', e.target.checked)
                            }
                        />
                        <span className="ms-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Remember me
                        </span>
                    </label>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-end">
                    {canResetPassword && (
                        <Link
                            href={route('password.request')}
                            className="rounded-md text-sm text-gray-600 underline hover:text-gray-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2"
                        >
                            Forgot your password?
                        </Link>
                    )}

                    <PrimaryButton className="ms-4" disabled={processing}>
                        Log in
                    </PrimaryButton>
                </div>
            </form>
        </GuestLayout>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Auth\Register.jsx]

import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import GuestLayout from '@/Layouts/GuestLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function Register() {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors, reset } = useForm({
        name: '',
        email: '',
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
    });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        post(route('register'), {
            onFinish: () => reset('password', 'password_confirmation'),
        });
    };

    return (
        <GuestLayout>
            <Head title="Register" />

            <form onSubmit={submit}>
                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="name" value="Name" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="name"
                        name="name"
                        value={data.name}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="name"
                        isFocused={true}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('name', e.target.value)}
                        required
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4">
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="email" value="Email" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="email"
                        type="email"
                        name="email"
                        value={data.email}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="username"
                        onChange={(e) => setData('email', e.target.value)}
                        required
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4">
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="password" value="Password" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password"
                        type="password"
                        name="password"
                        value={data.password}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                        onChange={(e) => setData('password', e.target.value)}
                        required
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4">
                    <InputLabel
                        htmlFor="password_confirmation"
                        value="Confirm Password"
                    />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password_confirmation"
                        type="password"
                        name="password_confirmation"
                        value={data.password_confirmation}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                        onChange={(e) =>
                            setData('password_confirmation', e.target.value)
                        }
                        required
                    />

                    <InputError
                        message={errors.password_confirmation}
                        className="mt-2"
                    />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-end">
                    <Link
                        href={route('login')}
                        className="rounded-md text-sm text-gray-600 underline hover:text-gray-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2"
                    >
                        Already registered?
                    </Link>

                    <PrimaryButton className="ms-4" disabled={processing}>
                        Register
                    </PrimaryButton>
                </div>
            </form>
        </GuestLayout>
    );
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Auth\ResetPassword.jsx]

import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import GuestLayout from '@/Layouts/GuestLayout';
import { Head, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function ResetPassword({ token, email }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors, reset } = useForm({
        token: token,
        email: email,
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
    });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        post(route('password.store'), {
            onFinish: () => reset('password', 'password_confirmation'),
        });
    };

    return (
        <GuestLayout>
            <Head title="Reset Password" />

            <form onSubmit={submit}>
                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="email" value="Email" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="email"
                        type="email"
                        name="email"
                        value={data.email}
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="username"
                        onChange={(e) => setData('email', e.target.value)}
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4">

import DangerButton from '@/Components/DangerButton';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import Modal from '@/Components/Modal';
import SecondaryButton from '@/Components/SecondaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import { useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';

export default function DeleteUserForm({ className = '' }) {
    const [confirmingUserDeletion, setConfirmingUserDeletion] = useState(false);
    const passwordInput = useRef();

    const {
        data,
        setData,
        delete: destroy,
        processing,
        reset,
        errors,
        clearErrors,
    } = useForm({
        password: '',
    });

    const confirmUserDeletion = () => {
        setConfirmingUserDeletion(true);
    };

    const deleteUser = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        destroy(route('profile.destroy'), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => closeModal(),
            onError: () => passwordInput.current.focus(),
            onFinish: () => reset(),
        });
    };

    const closeModal = () => {
        setConfirmingUserDeletion(false);

        clearErrors();
        reset();
    };

    return (
        <section className={`space-y-6 ${className}`}>
            <header>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                    Delete Account
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    Once your account is deleted, all of its resources and data
                    will be permanently deleted. Before deleting your account,
                    please download any data or information that you wish to
                    retain.
                </p>
            </header>

            <DangerButton onClick={confirmUserDeletion}>
                Delete Account
            </DangerButton>

            <Modal show={confirmingUserDeletion} onClose={closeModal}>
                <form onSubmit={deleteUser} className="p-6">
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                        Are you sure you want to delete your account?
                    </h2>

                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                        Once your account is deleted, all of its resources and
                        data will be permanently deleted. Please enter your
                        password to confirm you would like to permanently delete
                        your account.

                    <div className="mt-6">
                        <InputLabel
                            htmlFor="password"
                            value="Password"
                            className="sr-only"
                        />

                        <TextInput
                            id="password"
                            type="password"
                            name="password"
                            ref={passwordInput}
                            value={data.password}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                                setData('password', e.target.value)
                            }
                            className="mt-1 block w-3/4"
                            isFocused
                            placeholder="Password"
                        />

                        <InputError
                            message={errors.password}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Profile\Partials\UpdatePasswordForm.jsx]

import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import { Transition } from '@headlessui/react';
import { useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useRef } from 'react';

export default function UpdatePasswordForm({ className = '' }) {
    const passwordInput = useRef();
    const currentPasswordInput = useRef();

    const {
        data,
        setData,
        errors,
        put,
        reset,
        processing,
        recentlySuccessful,
    } = useForm({
        current_password: '',
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
    });

    const updatePassword = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        put(route('password.update'), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => reset(),
            onError: (errors) => {
                if (errors.password) {
                    reset('password', 'password_confirmation');
                    passwordInput.current.focus();
                }

                if (errors.current_password) {
                    reset('current_password');
                    currentPasswordInput.current.focus();
                }
            },
        });
    };

    return (
        <section className={className}>
            <header>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                    Update Password
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    Ensure your account is using a long, random password to stay
                    secure.
                </p>
            </header>

            <form onSubmit={updatePassword} className="mt-6 space-y-6">
                <div>
                    <InputLabel
                        htmlFor="current_password"
                        value="Current Password"
                    />

                    <TextInput
                        id="current_password"
                        ref={currentPasswordInput}
                        value={data.current_password}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                            setData('current_password', e.target.value)
                        }
                        type="password"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="current-password"
                    />

                    <InputError
                        message={errors.current_password}
                        className="mt-2"
                    />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="password" value="New Password" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password"
                        ref={passwordInput}
                        value={data.password}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('password', e.target.value)}
                        type="password"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <InputLabel
                        htmlFor="password_confirmation"
                        value="Confirm Password"
                    />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password_confirmation"
                        value={data.password_confirmation}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                            setData('password_confirmation', e.target.value)
                        }
                        type="password"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                    />

                    <InputError
                        message={errors.password_confirmation}
                        className="mt-2"
                    />
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <PrimaryButton disabled={processing}>Save</PrimaryButton>

                    <Transition
                        show={recentlySuccessful}
                        enter="transition ease-in-out"
                        enterFrom="opacity-0"
                        leave="transition ease-in-out"
                        leaveTo="opacity-0"
                    >
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Saved.
                        </p>
                    </Transition>
                </div>
            </form>
        </section>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\Pages\Dashboard.jsx]

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import svgCode from "../../../public/favicon.svg?raw";
import "./Dashboard.css";

const SHAPES = "path, circle, rect, ellipse, line, polygon, polyline";

/**
 * This is only test to make svg interactive with react
 * @returns
 */
export default function Dashboard() {
    const boxRef = useRef(null); // referenc of SVG
    const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null); // id of clicked item on logo
    const [data, setData] = useState(null); // data from Laravel

    // get data from laravel only once
    useEffect(() => {
        fetch("/dashboard-data")
            .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : null))
            .then(setData)
            .catch(() => setData(null));
    }, []);

    // if selected changed: put class is-active on selected item
    useEffect(() => {
        const box = boxRef.current;
        box.querySelectorAll(".is-active").forEach((el) =>
            el.classList.remove("is-active"),
        );
        if (selected) {
            box.querySelector(`#${CSS.escape(selected)}`)?.classList.add(
                "is-active",
            );
        }
    }, [selected]);

    // findout which part of the logo was clicked
    function handleClick(e) {
        const shape = e.target.closest(SHAPES);
        setSelected(shape?.id || null);
    }

    /**
     * ```text
     * fix favicon <svg> tag
     * from
     * <svg width="24" height="24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none">
     * to
     * <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
     * @param {*} raw
     * @returns
     */
    function fixSvg(raw) {
        const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(raw, "image/svg+xml");
        const svg = doc.documentElement;

        // ถ้าไม่มี viewBox ให้สร้างจาก width/height (เช่น 24 x 24 → "0 0 24 24")
        const w = parseFloat(svg.getAttribute("width")) || 24;
        const h = parseFloat(svg.getAttribute("height")) || 24;
        if (!svg.hasAttribute("viewBox")) {
            svg.setAttribute("viewBox", `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
        }

        // ลบขนาดตายตัวออก ให้ CSS เป็นคนคุมขนาด
        svg.removeAttribute("width");
        svg.removeAttribute("height");

        // ถ้าไม่มี fill ให้ใส่ fill="none"
        if (!svg.hasAttribute("fill")) {
            svg.setAttribute("fill", "none");
        }

        return new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(svg);
    }

    const svgHtml = fixSvg(svgCode);

    return (
        <div className="logo-screen">
            <div
                ref={boxRef}
                className="logo-box"
                onClick={handleClick}
                dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: svgHtml }}
            />

            {selected && (
                <div className="info-panel">
                    <p>
                        คลิกชิ้น: <b>{selected}</b>
                    </p>
                    {data && <p>ยอดขาย: {data.sales}</p>}
                    <button onClick={() => setSelected(null)}>ปิด</button>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\services\service.auth.jsx]

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
    logout as googleLogout,
    loginWithGoogle,
    startAuthListener,
} from "@/flows/login/google/google.login";

import { L } from "@/i18n";

export function openChromeFromLine() {
    const isLineBrowser = /Line/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    if (!isLineBrowser) return true;

    return new Promise((resolve) => {
        const ok = confirm(L.line_browser);

        if (ok) {
            const url =
                "googlechrome://navigate?url=" +
                encodeURIComponent(window.location.href);

            window.location.href = url;
        }

        resolve(false);
    });
}

/**
 * LOGIN
 */
export const login = async () => {
    return loginWithGoogle();
};

/**
 * LOGOUT
 */
export const logout = async () => {
    return googleLogout();
};

/**
 * MAIN AUTH HOOK
 */
export function useAuth() {
    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        const unsubscribe = startAuthListener({
            setUser: (u) => {
                setUser(u);
            },
        });

        return () => {
            unsubscribe?.();
        };
    }, []);

    return user;
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\services\service.firestore.js]

import {
    collection,
    getDocs,
    addDoc,
    doc,
    deleteDoc,
    updateDoc,
} from "firebase/firestore";

import { db } from "@/config/config.firebase";
// import { useDev } from "@/components/DevProvider";

/** we don't use firebase/storage now, because it costs.
 * til we have a customer who want to pay for our app, then we use storage
 * */
// import { getStorage, ref, uploadBytes, getDownloadURL } from "firebase/storage";

// const storage = getStorage();

/**
 * Image Upload (shared utility)
 */
// export const uploadImage = async (file) => {
//     const storageRef = ref(storage, "images/" + Date.now());
//     await uploadBytes(storageRef, file);
//     return await getDownloadURL(storageRef);
// };

/**
 * FIRESTORE FACTORY SERVICE
 * 1 instance = 1 collection (tableName)
 */
export const createFirestoreService = (tableName) => {
    /**
     * const { DEV } = useDev(); //tobe remove,
     * we will limit user add product
     * and DEV must confirm upload image and text before public
     */
    return {
        getRecords: async () => {
            const querySnapshot = await getDocs(collection(db, tableName));

            return querySnapshot.docs.map((doc) => ({
                id: doc.id,
                ...doc.data(),
            }));
        },

        addRecord: async (data) => {
            // if (!DEV) {
            //     return crypto.randomUUID();
            // }
            const docRef = await addDoc(collection(db, tableName), data);
            return docRef.id;
        },

        deleteRecord: async (id) => {
            // if (!DEV) {
            //     return;
            // }
            await deleteDoc(doc(db, tableName, id));
        },

        updateRecord: async (id, data) => {
            // if (!DEV) {
            //     return;
            // }
            await updateDoc(doc(db, tableName, id), data);
        },
    };
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\services\service.record.js]

// ตัวอย่าง Axios Service ฝั่ง React ที่จะเอามาแทนที่ service.firestore.js เดิม
import axios from "axios";

export const createRecordService = (tableName) => {
    return {
        getRecords: async () => {
            const res = await axios.get(`/api/records/${tableName}`);
            return res.data;
        },
        addRecord: async (data) => {
            const res = await axios.post(`/api/records/${tableName}`, data);
            return res.data.id;
        },
        updateRecord: async (id, data) => {
            await axios.put(`/api/records/${tableName}/${id}`, data);
        },
        deleteRecord: async (id) => {
            await axios.delete(`/api/records/${tableName}/${id}`);
        },
    };
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\services\service.user_old.js]

import { doc, getDoc, deleteDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "@/config/config.firebase";
import { signOut } from "firebase/auth";
import { auth } from "@/config/config.firebase";
import { L } from "@/i18n";

export const createUserService = () => {
    return {
        getMyProfile: async (uid) => {
            const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", uid));

            if (!snap.exists()) return null;

            return {
                id: snap.id,
                ...snap.data(),
            };
        },
    };
};

export const deleteUserProfile = async (uid) => {
    if (!uid) return;

    const confirmDelete = window.confirm(L.warn_deleting);

    if (!confirmDelete) return;

    await deleteDoc(doc(db, "users", uid));
    await signOut(auth);

    window.location.href = "/";
};

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\stores\store.record.js]

import { create } from "zustand";

import { createRecordService } from "@/services/service.record";

const createRecordStore = (tablename) => {
    const service = createRecordService(tablename);

    const { getRecords, addRecord, updateRecord, deleteRecord } = service;

    return create((set, get) => ({
        records: [],

        loadingId: null,
        isRecordLoading: false,
        recordError: null,

        /**
         * check if records already fetched,
         * no reload records everytime
         * when e.g. page mount/unmount
         */
        hasFetched: false,

        fetch: async () => {
            if (get().hasFetched) return;

            set({
                isRecordLoading: true,
                recordError: null,
            });

            try {
                const list = await getRecords();

                const sortedList = Array.isArray(list)
                    ? [...list].sort((a, b) => {
                          const timeA = Number(a?.created_at ?? 0);
                          const timeB = Number(b?.created_at ?? 0);
                          return timeB - timeA;
                      })
                    : [];

                set({
                    records: sortedList,
                    hasFetched: true,
                });
            } catch (error) {
                set({
                    recordError: error.message,
                });
            } finally {
                set({
                    isRecordLoading: false,
                });
            }
        },

        add: async (data) => {
            const newItem = {
                ...data,
                created_at: Date.now(),
            };

            const created = await addRecord(newItem);
            const id = created?.id ?? created?.data?.id ?? created;
            const recordData =
                created && typeof created === "object" && !Array.isArray(created)
                    ? { ...newItem, ...created, id }
                    : { id, ...newItem };

            set((state) => ({
                records: [recordData, ...state.records],
            }));

            return id;
        },

        update: async (id, data) => {
            const updated = await updateRecord(id, data);
            const patch =
                updated && typeof updated === "object" && !Array.isArray(updated)
                    ? { ...data, ...updated }
                    : data;

            set((state) => ({
                records: state.records.map((p) =>
                    p.id === id ? { ...p, ...patch } : p,
                ),
            }));
        },

        remove: async (id) => {
            set({ loadingId: id });

            try {
                await deleteRecord(id);

                set((state) => ({
                    records: state.records.filter((p) => p.id !== id),
                }));
            } finally {
                set({ loadingId: null });
            }
        },
    }));
};

export default createRecordStore;



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\stores\store.record_old.js]

import { create } from "zustand";

import { createFirestoreService } from "@/services/service.firestore";

const createRecordStore = (tablename) => {
    const service = createFirestoreService(tablename);

    const { getRecords, addRecord, updateRecord, deleteRecord } = service;

    return create((set, get) => ({
        records: [],

        loadingId: null,
        isRecordLoading: false, //waiting until loading finish
        recordError: null, // error e.g. no connection to db

        /**
         * check if records already fetched,
         * no reload records everytime
         * when e.g. page mount/unmount
         */
        hasFetched: false,

        fetch: async () => {
            if (get().hasFetched) return;

            set({
                isRecordLoading: true,
                recordError: null,
            });

            try {
                const list = await getRecords();

                list.sort((a, b) => b.created_at - a.created_at);

                set({
                    records: list,
                    hasFetched: true,
                });
            } catch (error) {
                set({
                    recordError: error.message,
                });
            } finally {
                set({
                    isRecordLoading: false,
                });
            }
        },

        add: async (data) => {
            const newItem = {
                ...data,
                created_at: Date.now(),
            };

            const id = await addRecord(newItem);

            set((state) => ({
                records: [{ id, ...newItem }, ...state.records],
            }));

            return id;
        },

        update: async (id, data) => {
            await updateRecord(id, data);

            set((state) => ({
                records: state.records.map((p) =>
                    p.id === id ? { ...p, ...data } : p,
                ),
            }));
        },

        remove: async (id) => {
            set({ loadingId: id });

            await deleteRecord(id);

            set((state) => ({
                records: state.records.filter((p) => p.id !== id),
                loadingId: null,
            }));
        },
    }));
};

export default createRecordStore;

/**
 * CssInjector is a dynamic runtime utility injection
 * need Observer for the targets html appearing
 * compare to tailwind does not need observer
 * because, tailwind class already exist in jsx
 *
 * ========================================a
 * IMPORT CSS
 * ========================================
 */

import "./CssInjector.css";

/**
 * ========================================
 * CSS INJECT TARGETS
 * ========================================
 */

// this components in App have same styles
const CSS_INJECT_TARGETS = {
    "btn-3d": [
        "sidebar-btn-menu",
        "sidebar-btn-item",
        "btn-setting-item",
        "crud-btn-save",
        "crud-btn-add",
        "crud-btn-edit",
        "crud-btn-delete",
        "crud-btn-cancel",
        "btn-setting",
        "ifm-icon-only",
        "btn-icon-only",
        "btn-open-shop-product",
        "btn-plus",
        "btn-minus",
        "btn-select-products",
        "btn-order",
        "btn-open-order-list",
    ],
    "bg-full": [
        // buttons
        "sidebar-btn-menu",
        "sidebar-btn-item",
        "btn-setting-item",
        "crud-btn-save",
        "crud-btn-add",
        "crud-btn-edit",
        "crud-btn-delete",
        "crud-btn-cancel",
        "btn-setting",
        "ifm-icon-only",
        "btn-icon-only",
        "btn-open-shop-product",
        "btn-plus",
        "btn-minus",
        "btn-select-products",
        "btn-order",
        "btn-open-order-list",

        // other surface
        "header",
        "sp-header",
        "sp-card-container",
        "sp-header-content",
        "card-container",
        "form-edit",
        "form-add",
    ],
    "surface-3d": [
        // buttons
        "sidebar-btn-menu",
        "sidebar-btn-item",
        "btn-setting-item",
        "crud-btn-save",
        "crud-btn-add",
        "crud-btn-edit",
        "crud-btn-delete",
        "crud-btn-cancel",
        "btn-setting",
        "btn-icon-only",
        "btn-open-shop-product",
        "btn-plus",
        "btn-minus",
        "btn-select-products",
        "sp-card-container",
        "btn-order",
        "btn-open-order-list",

        // other surface
        "header",
        "sp-header",
        "sp-header-content",
        "sp-header",
        "form-edit",
        "form-add",
    ],

    "surface-3d-card": [
        "card-container",
        "ifm-icon-only",
        "sp-header-content",
        "sp-header",
    ],
};

/**
 * ========================================
 * CORE INJECT / REMOVE
 * ========================================
 */

export function injectCss(className) {
    const targets = CSS_INJECT_TARGETS[className];
    if (!targets) return;

    targets.forEach((targetClass) => {
        const elements = document.querySelectorAll(`.${targetClass}`);

        elements.forEach((element) => {
            element.classList.add(className);
        });
    });
}

export function removeCss(className) {
    const targets = CSS_INJECT_TARGETS[className];
    if (!targets) return;

    targets.forEach((targetClass) => {
        const elements = document.querySelectorAll(`.${targetClass}`);

        elements.forEach((element) => {
            element.classList.remove(className);
        });
    });
}

/**
 * ========================================
 * APPLY ALL REGISTERED INJECTIONS
 * ========================================
 */

/**
 * [Description for applyAll]
 *
 * @return [type]
 *
 */
function applyAll() {
    Object.keys(CSS_INJECT_TARGETS).forEach((className) => {
        injectCss(className);
    });
}

/**
 * ========================================
 * MUTATION OBSERVER (SINGLE INSTANCE)
 * will inject when the targets appear
 * 
 * call useEffect from component that should observe
 * e.g. from App.jsx
 * 
 * useEffect(() => {
         initCssInjectorObserver();
     }, []);
 * 
 * ========================================
 */

let observerStarted = false;

export function initCssInjectorObserver() {
    if (observerStarted) return;
    observerStarted = true;

    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
        // Re-apply all injections whenever DOM changes
        applyAll();
    });

    observer.observe(document.body, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true,
    });

    // initial run
    applyAll();
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\utils\CURRENCY.js]

/**
 * sanitize currency input while typing
 * - allow numbers, dot, and comma
 * - convert comma to dot
 * - prevent multiple decimal points
 */ 
export const normalizeCurrencyInput = (value) => {
    // allow only numbers, dot, comma
    if (!/^[0-9.,]*$/.test(value)) {
        return null;
    }

    // convert comma to dot
    value = value.replace(/,/g, ".");

    // split decimal parts
    const parts = value.split(".");

    // prevent multiple dots
    if (parts.length > 2) {
        return null;
    } 

    // rebuild clean value
    let result = parts[0] ?? "";

    if (value.includes(".")) {
        result = parts[0] + "." + (parts[1] ?? "");
    }

    return result;
};

export function validateEmail(value) {
    if (!value || value.trim() === "") {
        return L.must_insert_data;
    }

    const isValidEmail = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

    if (!isValidEmail.test(value)) {
        return L.must_correct_email;
    }

    return "";
}

[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\utils\fontsizer.js]

export const getFontsizer = (value) => {
    const length = String(value).length;

    return length >= 13
        ? "count-xx-small"
        : length >= 11
          ? "count-x-small"
          : length >= 9
            ? "count-smaller"
            : length >= 7
              ? "count-small"
              : length >= 5
                ? "count-medium"
                : length >= 3
                  ? "count-x-large"
                  : "count-xx-large";
};

export const isOverflow = (value) => String(value).length > 9;



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\utils\image.js]

export const compressImage = async (file, maxSizeKB = 200) => {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const img = new Image();
    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onload = (e) => {
      img.src = e.target.result;
    };

    img.onload = () => {
      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

      // 🔥 ลดขนาด (max width 800px)
      const maxWidth = 800;
      let width = img.width;
      let height = img.height;

      if (width > maxWidth) {
        height *= maxWidth / width;
        width = maxWidth;
      }

      canvas.width = width;
      canvas.height = height;

      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

      let quality = 0.9;
      let base64;

      // 🔁 loop ลด quality จนขนาดเล็กพอ
      do {
        base64 = canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", quality);
        quality -= 0.05;
      } while (base64.length / 1024 > maxSizeKB && quality > 0.3);

      resolve(base64);
    };

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\utils\input.utils.js]

/**
 * sanitize currency input while typing
 * - allow numbers, dot, and comma
 * - convert comma to dot
 * - prevent multiple decimal points
 */
export const normalizeCurrencyInput = (value) => {
    // allow only numbers, dot, comma
    if (!/^[0-9.,]*$/.test(value)) {
        return null;
    }

    // convert comma to dot
    value = value.replace(/,/g, ".");

    // split decimal parts
    const parts = value.split(".");

    // prevent multiple dots
    if (parts.length > 2) {
        return null;
    }

    // rebuild clean value
    let result = parts[0] ?? "";

    if (value.includes(".")) {
        result = parts[0] + "." + (parts[1] ?? "");
    }

    return result;
};



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\utils\ui.validator.js]

import { L } from "@/i18n";

import { KEY, TYPE } from "@/features/record/record.config";

export function validateRequired(value) {
    if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined) {
        return L.must_insert_data;
    }

    if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim() === "") {
        return L.must_insert_data;
    }

    return "";
}

export function validateAmount(amount) {
    const value = Number(amount);

    // if (!amount) {  // cannot check like this , becaus amount=0 is valid
    if (amount === "" || amount === null || amount === undefined) {
        return L.must_insert_data;
    }

    if (isNaN(value)) {
        return L.must_number;
    }

    if (value < 0) {
        return L.must_positive;
    }

    return "";
}

export function validateEmail(value) {
    if (!value || value.trim() === "") {
        return L.must_insert_data;
    }

    const isValidEmail = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

    if (!isValidEmail.test(value)) {
        return L.must_correct_email;
    }

    return "";
}

export function validatePhone(value) {
    if (!value || value.trim() === "") {
        return L.must_insert_data;
    }

    /**
     * valid patterns:
     * +66812345678
     * 0812345678
     * 0012345678
     */
    const isValid = /^\+?[0-9]+$/.test(value);

    if (!isValid) {
        return L.must_phone_nr;
    }

    return "";
}

export function validateRecord(record, fields) {
    return fields.reduce((errors, field) => {
        const value = record[field.key];

        let error = "";

        const isEmpty = value === "" || value === null || value === undefined;

        /**
         * 1. REQUIRED (ONLY from config)
         */
        if (field.required && isEmpty) {
            errors[field.key] = validateRequired(value);
            return errors;
        }

        /**
         * 2. TYPE validation (only if not empty)
         */
        if (!isEmpty) {
            if (field.type === TYPE.NUMBER || field.type === TYPE.CURRENCY) {
                error = validateAmount(value);
            }

            if (field.type === TYPE.EMAIL) {
                error = validateEmail(value);
            }

            // if (field.type === TYPE.PHONE) {
            //     error = validatePhone(value);
            // }
        }

        errors[field.key] = error;

        return errors;
    }, {});
}



[FILE: C:\Users\o\.vscode\react\ecommerce\resources\js\App.jsx]

import { useState } from "react";

import { useDev } from "@/components/DevProvider";
import useAppBootstrap from "@/hooks/useAppBootstrap";

import Header from "@/components/Header";
import ProductPage from "@/features/product/product.page";
import ShopPage from "@/features/shop/shop.page";
import UserPage from "@/features/user/user.page";
import OrderPage from "@/features/order/order.page";
import SelectPage from "@/features/select/select.page";

import LoginPageGoogle from "@/flows/login/google/LoginPageGoogle";
import Sidebar from "@/components/Sidebar";
import ButtonSetting from "@/components/ButtonSetting";

export default function App() {
    const { DEV } = useDev();
    const [page, setPage] = useState("customers");
    const [showSidebar, setShowSidebar] = useState(true);
    const [headerConfig, setHeaderConfig] = useState({
        actions: null,
    });

    const handleNavigate = (value) => {
        if (!value) return;
        setPage(value);
        setShowSidebar(true);
    };

    const { theme, setTheme } = useAppBootstrap();

    const renderPage = () => {
        switch (page) {
            case "products":
                return <ProductPage setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig} />;

            case "orders":
                return <OrderPage setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig} />;

            case "customers":
                return <SelectPage setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig} />;

            case "shops":
                return <ShopPage setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig} />;

            case "users":
                return <UserPage setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig} />;

            default:
                return <SelectPage setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig} />;
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="app-shell">
            {!showSidebar && (
                <div
                    className="sidebar-backdrop"
                    onClick={() => setShowSidebar(true)}
                />
            )}
            <div className="header">
                <Header
                    config={headerConfig}
                    ButtonSetting={
                        <ButtonSetting
                            // open,
                            // onClose,
                            theme={theme}
                            setTheme={setTheme}
                        />
                    }
                />
            </div>

            {/* SIDEBAR (ChatGPT style but safe) */}
            <aside className={showSidebar ? "sidebar showSidebar" : "sidebar"}>
                <Sidebar
                    handleNavigate={handleNavigate}
                    showSidebar={showSidebar}
                    onToggle={() => setShowSidebar((v) => !v)}
                    setHeaderConfig={setHeaderConfig}
                    setPage={setPage}
                    setShowSidebar={setShowSidebar}
                />
            </aside>
            <div className="page-area">{renderPage()}</div>
        </div>
    );
}

Route::get('/', function () {
    return view('app'); // 👈 สั่งให้พอร์ต 8000 วิ่งมาเปิดไฟล์ resources/views/app.blade.php โดยตรง
});

Route::middleware('auth')->group(function () {
    Route::get('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'edit'])->name('profile.edit');
    Route::patch('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'update'])->name('profile.update');
    Route::delete('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'destroy'])->name('profile.destroy');
});

require __DIR__ . '/auth.php';
